TL;DR: 本研究在IFEval指令遵循校验框架下，测试了LLM是否存在自我偏好偏差（即模型拒绝自己输出文本的有效修正）。通过让模型先撰写违反指令约束的草稿，再由官方校验器确认修正方案有效后，分别以原作者身份和新模型身份评估是否接受修正。在4个中档模型家族的85次比较中，未发现显著自我偏好：作者拒绝有效修正的频率与新鲜模型基本一致（差距-5.1个百分点，置信区间[-12.9, +2.7]）。当作者拒绝有效修正时，97%的理由是发现修正缺陷而非偏好。但无法排除小于约13个百分点的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去研究发现LLM作为评判者时存在自我偏好偏差（偏好自己生成的文本），这引发了一个问题：模型是否会因为自我偏好而拒绝接受对自己写作的有效修正。本文旨在通过确定性验证器（而非另一个模型）来测试这种自我修正抵抗现象。

Method: 使用IFEval指令遵循校验框架：模型先写草稿→官方校验器确认草稿违反约束→确认候选编辑能修复问题→模型以原作者身份或新鲜模型身份决定是否接受编辑。在4个中档模型家族中进行85次作者vs新鲜模型比较。

Result: 未发现显著自我偏好偏差：作者拒绝有效修正的比例与新鲜模型基本持平（差距-5.1个百分点，95%置信区间[-12.9, +2.7]）。自我怀疑提示在规模扩大后未复现。当作者拒绝有效修正时，97%的理由是识别出修正的缺陷，而非偏好。

Conclusion: 在当前实验设置下，LLM没有表现出显著的自我偏好修正抵抗行为，拒绝修正主要是基于内容缺陷的客观判断而非自我偏好。但由于样本量限制，小于约13个百分点的效应无法被排除。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly review and revise text, including their own. A documented self-preference bias (models favoring their own generations when acting as judges) raises the question of whether models also resist valid corrections to their own writing. We test this in a setting where "valid" is decided not by another model but by a deterministic verifier: instruction-following revision on IFEval. A model writes a draft; the official IFEval checker confirms the draft violates a constraint and that a candidate edit fixes it; the model then accepts or rejects that edit either as the genuine in-context author or as a fresh model that sees the draft neutrally. Across four mid-tier model families and 85 author-versus-fresh comparisons, we find no detectable self-preference: authors reject verified-good fixes to their own drafts at essentially the same rate as fresh models judging the same drafts (gap -5.1 pp, 95% CI [-12.9, +2.7]). A self-skepticism hint from a smaller pilot did not replicate at scale. The one robust observation is qualitative: when authors do reject a verified-good fix, 97% of their stated reasons are flaw-catching rather than preference, that is, about the character of rejections, not an elevated rate. Effects smaller than ~13 pp cannot be excluded at this sample size.

</details>


### [145] [HydraHead: From Head-Level Functional Heterogeneity to Specialized Attention Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097)
*Zhentao Tan,Wei Chen,Jingyi Shen,Yao Liu,Xu Shen,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HydraHead，一种在注意力头维度上混合全注意力（FA）与线性注意力（LA）的新型架构。通过可解释性驱动的头选择策略和尺度归一化融合模块，结合三阶段迁移蒸馏训练，HydraHead在长上下文任务中优于其他混合设计，仅用15B训练数据即在512K上下文长度上比基线提升69%以上，逼近Qwen3.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力的二次复杂度是长上下文处理的关键瓶颈，现有开源混合模型多采用层级策略，但已有研究指出线性注意力与全注意力的集成存在固有困难，注意力混合的设计空间尚未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 可解释性驱动的头选择策略：识别检索关键的头，仅对这些头保留全注意力；(2) 尺度归一化融合模块：调和全注意力与线性注意力头输出之间的分布差异；(3) 三阶段迁移管道：利用参数复用和蒸馏，以最小训练开销实现高性能混合模型。

Result: 在统一训练设置下，HydraHead在长上下文任务中优于其他混合设计，同时保持强通用推理能力。通过可解释性驱动的头选择，以7:1的LA与FA比例达到了3:1层级混合模型的长上下文性能。仅训练15B token，在512K上下文长度上比基线提升69%以上，接近Qwen3.5。

Conclusion: 头维度为融合异构注意力信号提供了自然且原则性的粒度，头级混合具有显著的可扩展潜力。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention poses a critical bottleneck for long-context processing, spurring interest in hybrid attention designs. Most open-source hybrid models adopt a layer-wise strategy. Yet, prior work has noted the inherent difficulty of integrating Linear Attention (LA) with Full Attention (FA), suggesting that the design space of attention hybridization remains underexplored. To probe this space, we conduct interpretability analysis and observe that layers exhibit block-wise functional similarity, while individual heads within the same layer display distinct functional specialization despite sharing input features. This head-level heterogeneity suggests that the head dimension provides a natural and principled granularity for fusing heterogeneous attention signals. Building on this insight, we introduce HydraHead, a novel architecture that hybridizes FA and LA along the head axis. HydraHead features two key innovations: (1) an interpretability-driven selection strategy that identifies retrieval-critical heads and preserves FA only for them, and (2) a scale-normalized fusion module that reconciles the distributional gap between FA and LA head outputs. By leveraging a three-stage transfer pipeline with parameter reuse and distillation, we achieve high-performance hybrid models with minimal training overhead. Under a unified training setup, HydraHead outperforms other hybrid designs in long-context tasks while maintaining strong general reasoning. With interpretability-driven head selection, it matches a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid's long-context performance at a 7:1 LA-to-FA ratio. Crucially, trained on only 15B tokens, HydraHead achieves over 69% improvement over the baseline at 512K context length, approaching Qwen3.5, a leading model of comparable size with a native context length of 256K. This highlights the significant scaling potential of head-level hybridization.

</details>


### [146] [When Does Streaming Tool Use Help? Characterizing Tool-Intent Stabilization in Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20113)
*Elroy Galbraith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了流式检索增强生成（Streaming RAG）中工具查询的意图稳定化时机，提出了一种模型无关的延迟隐藏界限，并在CRAG基准上验证：在合理操作点下，73.9%的查询可实现实质性的延迟隐藏，其中21.3%的有利样本中95.2%可流式处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式RAG通过并行发出工具查询来降低用户感知延迟，但现有研究仅报告聚合收益，忽略了其收益本质上是查询内在的——只有当正确的工具查询在用户说完之前就可确定时，推测才有帮助。本文旨在隔离并量化这一属性（工具意图稳定化），并建立模型无关的理论界限。

Method: 在CRAG基准（1371个验证问题）上：(i) 测量稳定化的分布；(ii) 推导模型无关的界限H，表示可隐藏在用户剩余输入后的工具延迟比例，作为工具延迟L和输入节奏δ的函数；(iii) 在真实流式流水线上验证实际节省达到或超过该界限；(iv) 识别哪些查询属性可预测早期与晚期稳定化。研究无需模型训练，在普通CPU硬件上运行。

Result: 在L=600ms, δ=3词/秒, θ=0.8的操作点下，73.9%的查询可实现实质性延迟隐藏；其中21.3%的有利样本（黄金证据以原文形式存在且可被BM25检索）中95.2%可流式处理。有利样本的充分稳定化φ_suf在精确和宽松匹配下分别位于[0.26, 0.281]区间（均属早期）。问题类型产生显著但粗糙的早/晚分界（Kruskal-Wallis p=0.017, epsilon^2=0.04）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了流式RAG延迟隐藏的理论基础，证明了在合理操作点下大部分查询可实现延迟隐藏，并揭示了查询属性对稳定化时机的预测能力，可直接指导学习型推测触发器的成本效益决策。

Abstract: Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Streaming RAG) reduces user-perceived latency by issuing tool queries in parallel with ongoing user input, before the utterance is complete. Reported gains are aggregate, yet the mechanism's benefit is fundamentally query-intrinsic: speculation can only help when the correct tool query becomes determinable before the user stops speaking or typing. We isolate and measure this property -- tool-intent stabilization, the point in the input stream at which a speculative query's retrieval converges to the answer-bearing result. On the CRAG benchmark (1371 validation questions) we (i) measure the distribution of stabilization, (ii) derive a model-agnostic bound H on the portion of tool latency that can be hidden behind the user's remaining input, as a function of tool latency L and input cadence δ, (iii) validate against a working streaming pipeline that realized savings meet or exceed this bound, and (iv) identify which query properties predict early versus late stabilization. The study requires no model training and runs on commodity CPU hardware. We find that at a realistic operating point (L=600ms, δ=3w/s, θ=0.8), 73.9% of queries across the full benchmark admit substantial latency hiding -- a blended figure that mixes sufficiency stabilization on the 21.3% of questions where gold evidence is verbatim-present and BM25-retrievable (95.2% streamable on this favorable slice) with a grounding-free top-1-settling fallback on the remainder. On the favorable slice, φ_suf is bracketed to [0.26, 0.281] by exact and relaxed grounding -- both early. Question type produces a significant but coarse early/late split (Kruskal-Wallis p=0.017, epsilon^2=0.04), directly informing when a learned speculative trigger is worth its cost.

</details>


### [147] [From Texts to Scores: Tracing the Emergence of Essay Quality Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20152)
*Jiaxu Zuo,Mu You,Kaixin Lan,Tao Fang,Yujia Huo,Henghua Shen,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针、跨提示泛化、降维和神经元分析等方法，系统研究了8个LLM在三个数据集上的内部表征，发现LLM的隐藏表征中线性可解码地编码了作文质量信息，并识别出与评分高度相关的"作文评分神经元"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在自动作文评分中取得了显著进展，但其内部评分机制尚不明确，缺乏对LLM如何编码作文质量信息的理解。

Method: 使用线性探针、跨提示泛化分析、降维和神经元级分析，在8个LLM、三个数据集（ASAP++、CSEE、ENEM）上进行系统研究。

Result: 作文质量信息以线性可解码形式存在于LLM表征中；表征逐层涌现、跨提示策略鲁棒、部分跨提示迁移；非线性探针相比线性探针仅有边际改进；识别出与评分高度相关的"作文评分神经元"；这些神经元的层分布随作文长度系统性偏移。

Conclusion: LLM编码了与作文质量相关的结构化表征，为LLM-based AES系统的可解释性提供了新见解。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have substantially transformed Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet the internal mechanisms underlying LLM-based scoring remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically analyze the hidden representations of eight LLMs across two English essay datasets (ASAP++, CSEE) and one Portuguese dataset (ENEM). Using linear probing, cross-prompt generalization, dimensionality reduction, and neuron-level analyses, we find consistent evidence that essay quality information is encoded in a linearly accessible form within LLM representations. These representations emerge progressively across layers, remain robust across prompting strategies, and partially transfer across essay prompts despite differences in scoring rubrics. In addition, nonlinear probes provide only marginal and inconsistent improvements over linear probes, suggesting that most essay quality information is already linearly decodable. We further identify individual ``essay scoring neurons'' whose activations strongly correlate with essay scores and whose behavior is sensitive to targeted intervention. Moreover, the layer-wise distribution of these neurons systematically shifts with essay length, with longer essays relying more heavily on deeper layers. Overall, our findings provide evidence that LLMs encode structured representations related to essay quality and offer new insights into the interpretability of LLM-based AES systems.

</details>


### [148] [MedRLM: Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence for Long-Context Clinical Reasoning, Sensor-Guided Screening, Evidence-Grounded Decision Support, and Community-to-Tertiary Referral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20164)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedRLM，一种递归式多模态健康智能框架，通过将患者案例视为可递归检查的外部临床环境，协调多个专业智能体并引入临床证据图记忆与传感器引导的递归触发机制，实现长上下文的临床推理、传感器引导筛查和社区到三级转诊支持，旨在将医学AI从静态问答转向可审计、多模态和工作流感知的临床决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大语言模型和检索增强生成系统通常依赖单步提示或检索，在处理分散在长电子健康记录、医学影像、传感器流、指南和转诊约束中的临床证据时表现脆弱，无法满足需要推理异构和纵向患者信息而非回答孤立医学问题的真实临床决策支持需求。

Method: MedRLM将患者案例视为可递归检查、分解、检索、验证和综合的外部临床环境；协调针对临床文本、纵向EHR、医学影像、生理传感器信号、指南检索、不确定性审计和转诊规划的专门智能体；引入临床证据图记忆以连接患者特定观察与检索证据、标准化定义、传感器衍生生物标志物和转诊标准；设计传感器引导的递归触发机制在检测到异常生理或行为模式时激活更深层推理，以及不确定性门控优化支持高危及低置信案例的临床审查。

Result: 概述了使用公共和认证临床数据集（涵盖EHR、放射学、ECG、ICU时间序列和转诊代理结果）的真实数据评估设计。

Conclusion: MedRLM旨在将医学AI从静态问答推向可审计、多模态和工作流感知的临床决策支持，实现长上下文临床推理、传感器引导筛查和社区到三级转诊支持。

Abstract: Real-world clinical decision support requires reasoning over heterogeneous and longitudinal patient information rather than answering isolated medical questions. However, current medical large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems often rely on single-step prompting or retrieval, which can be fragile when clinical evidence is distributed across long electronic health records, medical images, sensor streams, guidelines, and referral constraints. This paper proposes MedRLM, a Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence framework for long-context clinical reasoning, sensor-guided screening, and community-to-tertiary referral support. Instead of compressing all patient information into one prompt, MedRLM treats the patient case as an external clinical environment that can be recursively inspected, decomposed, retrieved, verified, and synthesized. The framework coordinates specialized agents for clinical text, longitudinal EHR, medical imaging, physiological sensor signals, guideline retrieval, uncertainty auditing, and referral planning. It further introduces a Clinical Evidence Graph Memory to connect patient-specific observations with retrieved evidence, standardized definitions, sensor-derived biomarkers, and referral criteria. A sensor-guided recursive triggering mechanism activates deeper reasoning when abnormal physiological or behavioral patterns are detected, while uncertainty-gated refinement supports clinician review for high-risk or low-confidence cases. We also outline a real-data evaluation design using public and credentialed clinical datasets spanning EHR, radiology, ECG, ICU time series, and referral-proxy outcomes. MedRLM aims to move medical AI from static question answering toward auditable, multimodal, and workflow-aware clinical decision support.

</details>


### [149] [ReNikud: Audio-Supervised Hebrew Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20179)
*Maxim Melichov,Yakov Kolani,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReNikud方法，通过弱音频监督（ASR伪标签）和伪元音化架构（字符级对齐），在无需手工标注的情况下实现现代希伯来语G2P转换，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希伯来语是辅音音素文字（abjad），元音大多不书写，导致G2P转换存在严重歧义。传统方法依赖稀缺的元音标注数据（nikud），且无法反映自然口语发音和词汇重音，限制了TTS等应用。

Method: (1) 基于音素ASR的伪标注流水线，利用数千小时无标注希伯来语音频生成反映自然发音的音素转录；(2) 伪元音化架构，在每个字符位置预测IPA音素，强制实现字符级对齐作为归纳偏置。

Result: 在现有希伯来语G2P基准和新构建的针对口语希伯来语的MILIM基准上，ReNikud均超越了此前最先进方法。

Conclusion: ReNikud有效克服了希伯来语G2P中元音标注稀缺、无法反映自然口语发音等局限，推动了希伯来语TTS和语音技术的发展。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion for Modern Hebrew is needed for applications like text-to-speech (TTS), but is challenging due to the language's abjad writing system, which leaves vowels largely unwritten, creating substantial ambiguity. Standard approaches first predict vowel diacritics (nikud) to produce International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA) transcriptions, but this is limited: vocalization data is scarce and laborious to produce, it does not specify features such as lexical stress, and it reflects formal grammatical rules rather than everyday spoken pronunciation. Direct sequence-to-sequence IPA prediction, meanwhile, struggles on limited data and fails to exploit the character-level alignment characteristic of abjads. Our method, ReNikud, overcomes these limitations with two key insights: (1) Weak audio supervision via a phoneme-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) pseudo-labeling pipeline on thousands of hours of unlabeled Hebrew audio, yielding phonemic transcriptions that reflect natural spoken norms without manual annotation. (2) A pseudo-vocalization architecture that predicts IPA phonemes at each character position, enforcing character-level alignment as an inductive bias. Results on existing Hebrew G2P benchmarks and the new targeted MILIM benchmark for spoken Hebrew show that ReNikud surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods. We will release our code and trained models to support further work on Hebrew TTS and speech technologies.

</details>


### [150] [Pitch Spelling Jazz Lead Sheets, Solo Transcriptions, Classical Piano and Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20198)
*Augustin Bouquillard,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合优化音高拼写和调性估计的算法，通过模态阶段（最短路径搜索最小化变音记号）和调性阶段（全局调号与局部音名优化）两步处理，适用于爵士乐、传统音乐及古典乐谱的转录与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决音乐转录中音高拼写（音符命名）和调性估计（调号与局部音阶）的联合优化问题，尤其针对爵士独奏等复杂音乐场景，以生成符合音乐记谱规范的数字化乐谱。

Method: 两阶段优化：1) 模态阶段：通过最短路径搜索为每小节提出最可能的局部音阶，最小化需打印的变音记号数量；2) 调性阶段：基于局部音阶估计全局调号和音符名称，使整首乐曲的记谱最优。同时定义了爵士音阶间的距离度量。

Result: 在包含爵士乐（Real Book、爵士独奏和贝斯线）、传统曲调、钢琴及单音乐器古典乐谱的数据集上进行了评估，验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法最初为音乐转录（尤其是爵士独奏的音频转录与数字乐库构建）设计，也可推广至其他音乐记谱处理任务；新定义的爵士音阶距离对音乐学研究有参考价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for pitch spelling and key estimation. Given an input in MIDI-like format, containing information on note pitches (expressed in semitones relative to the lowest reference note) and bar boundaries, it estimates the appropriate note names, a global Key Signature, and a local scale for each bar. This related information elements are evaluated jointly during two stages of optimisation. During an initial 'modal' stage, a probable scale is proposed for each bar, minimising the number of accidentals to be printed in the printed score with a shortest-path search. Then, during a second stage called 'tonal', these local scales are used to estimate the Key Signature and note names that would result in the best musical notation for the entire piece. We present evaluations conducted on datasets comprising a variety of digital musical scores: jazz lead sheets taken from the Real Book, transcriptions of recordings of jazz soli and bass lines, traditional tunes, as well as classical scores for piano and monophonic instruments. Our procedure was originally designed for use in music transcription, specifically for building digital collections of jazz solos transcribed from audio recordings, for the purposes of music analysis, teaching and the preservation of cultural heritage. This method should also prove useful for other tasks related to the processing of musical notation. Furthermore, to this end, we have defined new distances between various common jazz scales, which may be of some interest to musicological studies.

</details>


### [151] [CzechDocs: A Multiway Parallel Dataset of Formatted Documents for Minority Languages in Czechia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20212)
*Josef Jon,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了CzechDocs多路平行数据集，包含HTML、DOCX和PDF格式的捷克语及少数语言文档，用于评估保留文档格式的机器翻译系统，并公开了验证集和评估工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译评估数据集大多忽略文档格式信息，缺乏支持格式保持翻译评估的多语言、多格式平行语料。

Method: 构建了涵盖捷克语及乌克兰语、英语、越南语、俄语等少数语言的多路平行文档数据集，包含HTML、DOCX、PDF三种格式，并在验证集上比较了多种格式保持机器翻译方法。

Result: 公开了验证集和评估工具包，预留了用于未来共享任务的测试集。

Conclusion: CzechDocs为格式保持的文档级机器翻译提供了标准化评估基准，推动相关研究方向的发展。

Abstract: We present CzechDocs, a multiway parallel dataset of formatted documents (HTML, DOCX, and PDF) covering Czech and minority languages used in Czechia-primarily Ukrainian and English, with smaller portions of Vietnamese, Russian and other languages. The dataset is designed to support the evaluation of machine translation systems that aim to preserve document formatting during translation. We provide a comparison of the most common approaches to format-preserving machine translation on a validation subset of the dataset. This validation split, together with the evaluation toolkit, is publicly released for further research. A held-out test split will be reserved for a future shared task focused on document-level translation with formatting preservation.

</details>


### [152] [Actionable Activation Directions for Detecting and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment Across Language Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20225)
*Abdul Rafay Syed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在四个指令微调模型族上对不安全代码进行微调，发现线性激活方向可以分离对齐与不对齐状态（99.6%）。模型内因果干预可减少代码泄露21-51点，跨架构迁移效果显著但缺乏特异性，揭示了双层特异性结构和不对称迁移拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在不安全代码微调后产生的对齐失效（emergent misalignment）是否对应一个跨架构共享的、因果可操作的激活空间方向，为模型审计与安全纠正提供理论基础。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.2-1B、Ministral-3-3B四个模型族上进行相同的微调（不安全代码），利用均值差方向（difference-in-means direction）分离对齐与不对齐激活；通过因果转向（causal steering）进行干预；使用岭回归映射实现跨架构方向迁移，并设置随机/正交方向控制组验证特异性。

Result: （1）均值差方向在每模型最终层实现99.6%的激活分离；（2）模型内因果转向降低代码泄露21-51点，安全代码控制证实内容特异性；（3）跨架构迁移（岭回归）将行为抑制提升至46点，但随机/正交方向表现相当，特异性失败；（4）发现两层特异性结构：模型内方向因果特异且可操作，跨模型方向因果真实但非特异；（5）不对称迁移拓扑：Gemma和Qwen为几何供体，Llama为受体。

Conclusion: 定义了线性跨架构纠正的极限：模型内方向可用于审计与纠正，但跨架构方向缺乏特异性，无法可靠迁移；建议采用模型内探针进行审计。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on insecure code induces emergent misalignment with poorly understood internal structure. We investigate whether this misalignment corresponds to a causally actionable activation-space direction shared across architectures. Across four instruction-tuned model families (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B, Ministral-3-3B) finetuned identically, a difference-in-means direction achieves 99.6% separation of aligned and misaligned activations at each model's final layer. Causal steering by subtracting this direction reduces code spillover by 21-51 points, while a secure-code control confirms content specificity. Cross-architecture transfer via ridge regression maps yields large behavioral suppression (up to 46 points) but fails specificity controls as random and orthogonal directions perform comparably. We identify a two-tier specificity structure: within-model directions are causally specific and actionable; cross-model directions are causally real but non-specific. An asymmetric transfer topology emerges, with Gemma and Qwen acting as geometric donors and Llama as a receiver. These findings define the limits of linear cross-architecture correction and recommend within-model probing for auditing.

</details>


### [153] [The Register Gap: A Meaning Intelligence Framework for Nigerian Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20255)
*Celestine Achi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出意义智能框架（MIF），一个九维度注释与评估体系，用于分离尼日利亚公共话语的表层情感与真实交际意图。研究发现AI系统的主要失败模式不是翻译失败而是语境失败。基于30项校准数据集评估Gemini 2.5 Flash模型，结果显示零样本下体裁分类准确率仅33.3%，在MIF框架提示下提升至73.3%（+40点），综合意义智能评分从73.2提升至78.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尼日利亚语言基准（如NaijaSenti、AfriSenti）仅将情感分类视为三元极性任务（正面、负面、中性），无法捕捉尼日利亚话语中依赖说话者、受众和情境的语用力量，导致AI系统出现"语境失败"而非翻译失败。

Method: 构建MIF九维度评分体系（体裁、表层情感、真实意图、讽刺、编码潜台词、风险等级、注释者置信度、说话者情绪、建议沟通行动），创建30项校准数据集（涵盖标准英语、尼日利亚英语、尼日利亚皮钦语及混合语码），使用Gemini 2.5 Flash在零样本和MIF框架提示条件下进行评估。

Result: 零样本体裁分类准确率33.3%，MIF框架提示下提升至73.3%（+40点）；意义智能评分从73.2升至78.6（+5.4点）；编码潜台词检测提升10点；战略行动推荐提升10.3点。

Conclusion: MIF框架能有效提升AI对尼日利亚公共话语中语境信息的捕捉能力，尤其显著改善体裁识别、潜台词检测和行动推荐。研究公开了框架规范、注释指南和30项校准集以支持可复现性。

Abstract: We introduce the Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF), a nine-dimension annotation and evaluation schema for Nigerian public discourse that separates surface sentiment from true communicative intent. Existing benchmarks for Nigerian languages, including NaijaSenti and AfriSenti, treat sentiment classification as a three-way polarity task (positive, negative, neutral). We argue that the dominant failure mode of AI systems on Nigerian discourse is not translation failure but context failure: the same utterance carries opposite pragmatic force depending on speaker, audience, and situation. The MIF operationalises this insight across nine scored dimensions: register, surface sentiment, true intent, irony, coded subtext, risk tier, annotator confidence, speaker emotion, and recommended communications action. We construct a 30-item calibration dataset spanning Standard English, Nigerian English, Nigerian Pidgin, and code-mixed registers, and evaluate a frontier language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) under zero-shot and schema-informed prompting conditions. The headline finding is the Register Gap: zero-shot register classification accuracy is 33.3%, rising to 73.3% (+40 points) when the model receives the MIF schema in-context. The composite Meaning Intelligence Score increases by 5.4 points (73.2 to 78.6) under schema-informed prompting, with the largest practical gains in register identification, coded-subtext detection (+10 points), and strategic action recommendation (+10.3 points). We release the framework specification, annotation guidelines, and the 30-item public calibration set to support reproducibility, while retaining a private holdout corpus for contamination-protected evaluation.

</details>


### [154] [PsyScore: A Psychometrically-Aware Framework for Trait-Adaptive Essay Scoring and ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20287)
*Wei Xia,Jin Wu,Haoran Shi,Xiangyu Wang,Chanjin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyScore是一个心理测量感知框架，通过共享潜在能力表征将诊断性评估与教学脚手架整合，同时实现可靠评分和个性化反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将评分和反馈作为分离组件：神经评分模型可解释性有限，而基于LLM的反馈通常对学习者能力水平不敏感，导致评估与教学支持之间的碎片化。

Method: 提出PsyScore框架，包含三个模块：（1）Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer，将Graded Partial Credit Model融入神经架构以精确估计学生能力；（2）ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator，根据诊断的能力参数调节多智能体反馈策略；（3）Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy，通过成对偏好判断和学生修订模拟评估反馈质量。

Result: 在ASAP++数据集上，PsyScore在保持竞争性评分性能的同时，提供了更具教学契合度的反馈。

Conclusion: PsyScore证明了将心理测量学原理与神经语言模型相结合，能够实现评分精确性与反馈教学性的统一，为自动化作文评分的评估-教学一体化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Effective Automated Essay Scoring (AES) are expected to support both reliable assessment and actionable instructional feedback. However, existing approaches often treat scoring and feedback as separate components: neural scoring models provide limited interpretability, while Large Language Model (LLM)-based feedback is typically insensitive to learners proficiency levels. To address this fragmentation, this work proposes PsyScore, a psychometrically-aware framework that integrates diagnostic assessment with instructional scaffolding through a shared latent ability representation. PsyScore comprises three key modules: a Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer that incorporates the Graded Partial Credit Model (GPCM) into a neural architecture, enabling the precise estimation of student ability while maintaining psychometric interpretability, a ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator, which conditions multi-agent feedback strategies on the diagnosed ability parameter to adapt instructional focus across different proficiency levels, and a Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy that assesses feedback quality via pairwise preference judgements and student revision simulations. Experiments on the ASAP++ dataset demonstrate that PsyScore achieves competitive scoring performance while providing more pedagogically aligned feedback.

</details>


### [155] [CATCH-ME if you RAG: a dataset of Contextually Annotated multi-Turn Counterspeech against Hate and Misinformation Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20369)
*Helena Bonaldi,Genoveffa Martone,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了第一个大规模、专家策划的多语言对话数据集，专门针对仇恨言论与错误信息重叠的领域，涵盖5种语言和7个边缘化群体，并包含事实核查文章和NGO报告的文档级与片段级标注，可直接用于RAG系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NLP研究主要将仇恨言论和错误信息分开处理，而现实中的对话往往跨越多个轮次和语言；当前的计数器话语数据集稀缺且局限于单轮英文对话，零样本模型生成的内容重复且模糊，亟需高质量、多语言的示例来引导模型生成更具说服力和事实依据的回应。

Method: 构建了首个大规模、专家策划的多语言对话数据集，涵盖5种语言、7个边缘化群体，对话内容锚定于经过验证的外部知识（如事实核查文章和NGO报告），并包含文档级和片段级的跨度标注，使其可直接用于RAG系统。

Result: 创建了一个多语言、多轮对话的数据集，填补了仇恨言论与错误信息重叠领域的高质量计数器话语数据空白，支持训练和评估更具说服力、事实依据的计数器话语模型。

Conclusion: 该数据集为仇恨言论与错误信息重叠场景下的计数器话语生成提供了前所未有的多语言、多轮对话资源，通过事实锚定和细粒度标注，有望显著提升LLM在该复杂任务上的表现。

Abstract: Online hate speech and misinformation frequently overlap, yet NLP research has mainly treated them in isolation. While LLMs represent a scalable solution for assisting humans in the generation of counterspeech for both threats, zero-shot models frequently generate repetitive and vague responses, underscoring the need for high-quality examples to steer model generation. However, existing counterspeech datasets against the overlap of hate and misinformation are scarce and limited to single-turn English dialogues, while real-life interactions span across multiple turns and languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first large-scale, expert-curated, multilingual dataset of dialogues tackling the intersection of hate and misinformation. To ensure factual grounding, the dialogues are also anchored in verified external knowledge (i.e., fact-checking articles and NGO reports) and include document- and chunk-level span annotations, making it directly applicable for RAG systems. Covering five languages and targeting hate directed at seven marginalized groups, this novel resource enables the training and evaluation of more persuasive, factually grounded counterspeech models.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20487)
*Shu Yao,Yuhua Luo,Qian Long,Jingru Fan,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuheng Wang,Lin Wu,Yufan Dang,Huatao Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出H-RePlan分层重规划框架和HeraBench故障注入基准，通过设备本地策略恢复与编排器全局重规划的分离机制，在多设备多应用场景下显著提升任务完成率和指令遵循度，同时降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨设备代理系统在运行时故障恢复方面粒度粗糙，无法区分"可在当前设备内修复的失败"和"需跨设备重新规划的失败"，缺乏对设备本地策略空间的系统建模。

Method: 提出H-RePlan框架，为每个设备配备可互换的执行策略，通过紧凑的跨层故障抽象将设备本地策略恢复与编排器级全局重规划分离；构建HeraBench基准，在Linux/Android设备上注入策略级和设备级故障。

Result: H-RePlan在完成率、指令遵循度和完美通过率上显著优于单策略和粗粒度多设备基线，同时降低了实现端到端可靠成功所需的token成本。

Conclusion: 范围感知的分层恢复机制对于鲁棒的多设备代理执行至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.

</details>


### [157] [StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20527)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Timo Cavelius,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Samantha Dalal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StylisticBias基准，通过控制身份固定、逐个改变视觉属性的方法，系统评估多模态大模型在25个二元社会判断场景中的属性级社会偏见。研究发现年龄和体型主导身份级效应，约15个视觉属性解释了近80%的偏见变异，且偏见在与社会经济及风格相关的判断中最强烈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大模型社会偏见研究往往比较不同个体或群体，难以区分外貌效应与身份差异的影响。需要一种能够隔离特定视觉属性、精确测量属性级偏见的方法。

Method: 生成500张逼真的基础人脸，每张人脸创建约50个单属性变体（共约25K张图像），保持身份固定而每次只改变一个视觉属性。在25个二元社会判断场景中评估6个多模态大模型，测量特定视觉线索如何改变模型判断。

Result: 年龄和体型的身份级效应最显著；时尚风格等视觉线索驱动最大的属性级偏见；约15个属性解释了近80%的总变异；偏见在与社会经济和风格相关的判断中强度最大。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型的视觉偏见集中在少数视觉属性上，且偏见强度与判断的语义对齐程度相关。StylisticBias可作为细粒度偏见评估的基准，有助于理解并缓解模型中的视觉社会偏见。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [LLM-Mediated Human-AI Interaction in Search and Rescue: Impact of Expertise on Attentional Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19514)
*Elahe Oveisi,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究人机协作中LLM引导对任务表现和认知的影响，通过搜索救援仿真实验发现：LLM提升了任务效率但未增加总救人数量；眼动数据显示注意力引导权衡（视觉资源转向聊天界面且瞳孔变异性增大）；专家保持环境扫描的"验证循环"，而新手表现出被动依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统（特别是LLM）在实时复杂任务中提供上下文感知指导时，如何影响人类认知、注意力分配和决策行为，以及这种影响如何随操作者专业水平变化。

Method: 在模拟搜索救援环境中，设置两种LLM引导条件与无LLM基线条件进行对比；采用多层面分析：任务绩效指标、眼动追踪（注意力分配）和规划行为（结构化决策适应）。

Result: LLM引导提升了任务效率（更高奖励和每步救人数量）但未提升总救人数；眼动数据显示注意力从环境转向聊天界面，瞳孔大小变异性增加；专业水平调节该效应：新手表现出被动AI依赖，专家维持"验证循环"持续扫描环境。

Conclusion: LLM介导的人机协作效能取决于操作者将AI指导与地面实况交叉验证以维持情境意识的能力。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [159] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对19名计算机专业大学生的眼动追踪与访谈研究，发现学生在使用多表征编程可视化工具时，近半时间仍聚焦代码；选择性地忽略可视化源于三个关键因素：自主权（控制认知努力）、表征适配（设计相同但感受不同）以及合法性（将隐喻视为幼稚或不严谨）。研究建议将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换抽象层级、并注重工具的学科正当性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序可视化工具虽广泛用于辅助编程初学者，但学生常常忽略或抗拒这些精心设计的视觉支架。尽管关于多重外部表征(MERs)的研究提供了协调多个视图的认知设计原则，但影响学习者如何参与可用表征的因素尚不明确。因此，本研究旨在探索学生在使用多表征编程工具时，是什么因素塑造了他们对不同表征的选择性参与。

Method: 采用被试内设计，招募19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生。学生使用包含同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征探测工具以及Python Tutor，完成范围、while循环和链表等任务的边想边说、反思性访谈和基于网络摄像头的眼动追踪。

Result: 眼动分析显示，尽管有可视化支架可用，学生仍有近一半时间专注于代码。缺乏先验经验的学生更倾向于锚定在代码上，且极少参与隐喻视图。访谈确定了塑造选择性参与的三个因素：自主权（学生寻求对认知努力的控制而非简单减少）、表征适配（相同设计在不同语境中被视为有用或令人不知所措）以及合法性（部分学生避免使用他们认为幼稚或不够严谨的隐喻性支架）。

Conclusion: 多表征编程教育工具需要关注情感和社会因素，而不仅仅是认知设计。实践建议包括将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换的抽象层级、以及以传达学科正当性的方式框架化工具。更广泛地，这些主题有助于解释为何认知上合理的可视化工具可能无法吸引它们旨在帮助的学生。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [160] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对循环建筑经济中回收材料带来的现场即兴施工挑战，本文提出"建筑漂移"概念，通过ReShelter案例建立表征物理状态与数字模型偏差的分类体系（场地照料、适配搜寻、材料解读、测量标记、社区协调），并开发了Pentimento工具——利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术对现场改造进行时空语义表征，降低材料再利用障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环建筑经济中回收材料存在使用前史和未来建筑中的后史，但这类材料带来的不确定性需要现场即兴改造，而缺乏文档记录使得合作者、评估者和继承者无法获取继续使用、评估和再利用所需的信息，导致回收材料的复用难以记录和规模化推广。

Method: 通过ReShelter（森林中建造的回收木材展馆）案例研究，建立表征建筑漂移的分类体系；开发Pentimento工具，结合视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术，对现场改造进行空间、时间和语义层面的表征，并与设计模型建立关联。

Result: 提出了建筑漂移的五个分类维度：场地照料、适配搜寻、材料解读、测量标记、社区协调；实现了Pentimento工具，使各利益相关方能够导航材料历史，降低材料再利用的障碍。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为支持回收材料施工中必不可少的现场即兴改造的计算工具开辟了路径，推动了更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [161] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Syndesmoscope，一个并置多种不变性网络可视化视图的交互系统，包括力导向图、密度梯度、测地偏心率和谱二分视图，并引入新的不变性可视化方法 kSnakes，通过跳跃式高亮和跳房子式遍历两种交互操作，帮助用户从多个角度探索网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络可视化（节点链接图和邻接矩阵）的视觉模式高度依赖布局或排序算法，导致同一拓扑结构可能呈现不同图案。不变性可视化能稳定呈现相同拓扑的相同视觉模式，但研究不足且未集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 设计并实现 Syndesmoscope 系统，并置力导向视图和三种基于图论属性的可解释几何布局视图（密度梯度、测地偏心率和谱二分），并引入新的不变性可视化方法 kSnakes（基于密度分解）。支持两种交互：leapfrogging（链接高亮）和 hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历扩展选择）。

Result: 在包含72个不同网络的语料库上展示了系统有效性，证明这些交互能揭示单一视图无法获取的网络模式。

Conclusion: 不变性可视化结合多视图并置与拓扑感知交互，能有效辅助网络模式发现，值得进一步集成到可视化工具中。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [162] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过16名参与者的实证研究，探讨用户如何使用AI驱动的"氛围编码"工具实现数据可视化，分析其在提示、评估和迭代中的实践与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化对数据分析和沟通至关重要，但创建富有表现力的可视化仍需要大量人工劳动。尽管AI驱动的"氛围编码"工具通过自然语言交互降低了门槛，但可视化实现需要用户意图与视觉表示之间的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发实践可能存在差异，因此需要深入研究用户如何使用这些工具。

Method: 招募16名不同专业水平的参与者进行实证研究，参与者需完成两项可视化任务并进行半结构化访谈，通过分析其提示、评估和迭代过程来总结实践行为与面临挑战。

Result: 研究刻画了用户在使用氛围编码工具进行可视化实现时，在提示、评估和迭代阶段所采用的不同实践策略，并揭示了他们在整个过程中遇到的各种挑战。

Conclusion: 用户在使用AI驱动的氛围编码工具进行可视化实现时存在多样化的实践方式，且面临特定挑战，提示需要进一步优化工具设计以更好地支持可视化开发中的用户意图与视觉表示对齐。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [163] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过5位穆斯林女性的半结构化访谈，探讨了数字工具中《古兰经》、圣训和先知传记分离阅读的问题，提出了"分层语境性"概念，要求语境扩展必须平衡解释责任、 devotional flow 以及跨设备和学习强度的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习通常需要同时阅读《古兰经》、圣训和先知传记，但现有数字工具将这些资源分散在不同的应用、屏幕和搜索路径中，破坏了学习的连贯性。研究旨在从人机交互角度理解这一问题。

Method: 从在线伊斯兰学习社区招募5位穆斯林女性，进行半结构化访谈，通过性别化数字宗教、认知信任和无缝学习三个理论视角分析访谈数据。

Result: 识别出五个主题：上下文理解、真实性、界面杂乱、学习模式和指导功能。参与者希望阅读时能获取语境信息，但必须满足可信、可选且不打断阅读的条件。

Conclusion: 提出"分层语境性"作为HCI领域的新概念：语境扩展需要在解释责任、 devotional flow 以及跨设备和学习强度的连续性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [164] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGUI-Agent，一种面向长时域移动GUI任务的智能体，通过将上下文管理建模为首要动作（ConAct），维护折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态和近期步骤记录三个结构化字段，避免提示爆炸和关键信息稀释。基于2,956条轨迹数据集MemGUI-3K训练8B模型，在MemGUI-Bench和MobileWorld上达到最优8B性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的移动GUI智能体在短期任务上表现良好，但在需要跨多步骤和跨应用保留中间事实的长时域任务中不可靠。根本原因是ReAct-style提示方式被动累积每步记录，导致提示爆炸和关键跨应用事实被稀释。

Method: 提出Context-as-Action (ConAct)范式，将上下文管理建模为与选择UI动作同策略的一等动作。维护三个结构化上下文字段：折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态和近期步骤记录，在保留关键UI事实的同时保持上下文紧凑。构建包含2,956条完整ConAct标注轨迹的MemGUI-3K数据集用于监督训练和离线分析。

Result: 在MemGUI-3K上训练的8B模型MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上达到最佳开源8B性能，并能泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准测试。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理建模为可学习的主动动作而非被动累积，能有效提升移动GUI智能体在长时域任务中的可靠性，并且该方法在不同模型规模下均可学习。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact. To make proactive context management learnable across model scales, we construct MemGUI-3K, a 2,956-trajectory dataset with full ConAct annotations for supervised training and offline analysis. Training an 8B model on MemGUI-3K produces MemGUI-8B-SFT, an 8B MemGUI-Agent that achieves the best open-data 8B performance on MemGUI-Bench and generalizes to the out-of-distribution MobileWorld benchmark. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://memgui-agent.github.io/.

</details>


### [165] [MobileForge: Annotation-Free Adaptation for Mobile GUI Agents with Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19930)
*Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Gao Wu,Yiwen Yin,Mading Li,Liang Liu,Congxiao Liu,Zhang Qi,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MobileForge是一个无需人工标注的移动GUI智能体适配系统，通过MobileGym环境和HiFPO策略优化，使Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3，适配后的ForgeOwl-8B更达到77.6% Pass@3，成为最强开源移动GUI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体适配真实应用成本高昂，因为移动应用数量庞大、更新频繁，难以通过人工编写任务、演示或奖励标签覆盖。现有免标注方法缺乏统一框架连接目标应用探索、课程挖掘、回滚执行和反馈，且策略优化常依赖孤立回滚和粗粒度奖励，难以转化为可靠的改进信号。

Method: MobileForge由两个核心组件构成：1) MobileGym——将任务生成和回滚评估建立在真实移动应用交互之上；2) 分层反馈引导策略优化(HiFPO)——将轨迹结果、步骤级过程反馈和纠正提示转化为提示上下文化的步骤级GRPO更新。

Result: MobileForge仅使用自动生成的免标注适应数据，将Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上提升至67.2% Pass@3（接近闭数据GUI专用模型GUI-Owl-1.5-8B的69.0%）；适配后的ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3，并在域外MobileWorld GUI-only分割上实现41.0%成功率。

Conclusion: MobileForge建立了当前评估中最强的开源数据移动GUI智能体，证明了无需人工标注即可实现接近甚至超越闭数据专用模型的性能。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress in UI understanding and action execution, but adapting them to real target apps remains costly because mobile apps are numerous, frequently updated, and hard to cover with human-written tasks, demonstrations, or reward labels. Existing annotation-free GUI learning reduces manual supervision, yet lacks a unified substrate connecting target-app exploration, curriculum mining, rollout execution, and feedback, while policy optimization often relies on isolated rollouts and coarse rewards that are hard to convert into reliable improvement signals. We present MobileForge, an annotation-free adaptation system for mobile GUI agents. MobileForge consists of MobileGym, which grounds task generation and rollout evaluation in real mobile app interaction, and Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization (HiFPO), which turns trajectory outcomes, step-level process feedback, and corrective hints into hint-contextualized step-level GRPO updates. Using only automatically generated annotation-free adaptation data, MobileForge adapts Qwen3-VL-8B to 67.2% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld, close to the closed-data GUI-specialized GUI-Owl-1.5-8B base model at 69.0%. The MobileForge-adapted ForgeOwl-8B further reaches 77.6% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld and 41.0% success on the out-of-domain MobileWorld GUI-only split, establishing the strongest open-data mobile GUI agent in our evaluation. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://mobile-forge.github.io/.

</details>


### [166] [AI Conversational Interviewing: Scaling Up Semi-Structured and In-depth Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20064)
*Alexander Wuttke,Max Melchior Lang,Christopher Klamm,Quirin Würschinger,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对话式访谈（AI Conversational Interviewing）作为在规模化条件下收集开放式民意数据的方法，通过结合AI主导的访谈与标准化调查，在571名受访者中验证了该方法在捕捉深层思考、受访者评价和实现可行性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统民意研究在深度与规模之间存在权衡：标准化调查可大规模测量但受限于预设类别，无法捕捉公众态度中的多样化意外因素；开放式访谈能提供丰富见解但依赖人工访谈员难以规模化。该研究旨在解决这一方法论困境。

Method: 研究设计了AI主导的对话式访谈与标准化调查相结合的实验，通过Prolific和Payback Panel招募571名受访者，实验比较了语音、文本和自由选择三种访谈模式，并释放了开放数据和开源管线材料。

Result: AI对话式访谈能够揭示标准化问卷无法捕捉的深层考量与推理过程，例如在态度水平相似的不同子群体中存在显著不同的移民心理模型。完成访谈的受访者对AI访谈的评价在各模式下均达到或超过标准化调查，但完成率因条件而异。

Conclusion: AI对话式访谈是社会科学工具箱中可行且有价值的补充方法，通过释放开放数据和开源材料，该研究为利用人工智能扩展民意测量方法的相关文献做出了贡献。

Abstract: Public opinion research has long faced a trade-off between depth and scale: standardized surveys enable large-scale measurement but restrict respondents to researcher-defined categories, obscuring the diversity of unexpected considerations that underlie public sentiment. More conversational interviews provide richer insights through open-ended probing, but their reliance on trained human interviewers has kept them difficult to scale. This study introduces AI Conversational Interviewing as a method for collecting open-ended public opinion data at scale, pursuing three objectives: to demonstrate the analytical value of conversational text data for questions beyond the reach of closed-ended items; to assess the method's practical viability through participants' own evaluations; and to inform implementation by experimentally comparing voice-based, chat-based, and free-choice interview modes. We conducted a study combining an AI-led interview with a standardized survey on migration policy among 571 respondents recruited via Prolific and Payback Panel. The findings establish AI Conversational Interviewing as a viable and valuable addition to the social-science toolkit. The conversational transcripts surface considerations and reasoning that a comprehensive standardized battery does not capture such as markedly different mental models of migration among subgroups with similar attitudes levels. Among respondents who completed the interview, evaluations of the AI interview were at or above those of the standardized survey across modes, although completion itself varied by condition. By releasing open data and open-source pipeline materials, the study contributes to a growing literature on harnessing artificial intelligence to expand the methods of public opinion measurement.

</details>


### [167] [Editorial Alignment: A Participatory Approach to Engaging Editorial Expertise in LLM-mediated Knowledge Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20258)
*Simon Aagaard Enni,Malthe Stavning Erslev,Karl-Emil Kjær Bilstrup,Kristoffer Laigaard Nielbo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在LLM驱动的信息服务背景下，公共知识机构的编辑职能如何通过参与式设计实现与LLM界面的对齐（编辑对齐），以维护编辑权威和标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的信息服务威胁到公共知识机构的编辑职能，预训练LLM已经嵌入了商业开发者的价值观，亟需探索编辑如何参与重新对齐LLM接口以维护编辑标准。

Method: 通过设计工作坊的案例研究，与北欧公共知识机构合作设计和实现了一个LLM赋能的百科全书接口，将编辑对齐作为参与式AI中的设计实践。

Result: 提出了编辑对齐作为一种设计实践，将AI对齐视为设计过程，并将编辑标准定位为将编辑实践和价值转化为技术实现对齐目标的设计制品。

Conclusion: 编辑对齐可以为持续参与创造空间，并在LLM中介的知识传播中赋予编辑更多自主权。

Abstract: The emergence of LLM-driven information services is reshaping the conditions under which public knowledge institutions operate, threatening to absorb the editorial function these institutions exist to exercise. While LLMs offer powerful new affordances for knowledge dissemination, editorial authority is challenged by pretrained LLMs that arrive already aligned with the values and dissemination strategies of their commercial developers. This paper investigates editor participation in re-aligning LLM interfaces to editorial standards through design workshops, in a case study where we design and implement an LLM-enabled encyclopedia interface with a Nordic public knowledge institution. We introduce editorial alignment as a design practice within Participatory AI, framing AI alignment as a design process and positioning the editorial standard as a design artefact that translates editorial practice and values into alignment objectives for technical implementation. Last, we discuss how editorial alignment can create space for ongoing participation and give editors agency in LLM-mediated knowledge dissemination.

</details>


### [168] [Organizing in the Digital Age: Understanding Community, Challenges, and Consequences in Digitally-facilitated Labor Organizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20375)
*Frederick Reiber,Alishah Chator,Dana Calacci,Allison McDonald*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对17次定性访谈，深入分析了美国工会工作者如何利用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台进行劳工组织，发现数字工具虽对当代劳工成功至关重要，但也带来了技术安全、信息过载和建立信任等新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代美国劳动力高度分散，需要使用数字通信工具来弥合工会组织中的时空差距，因此需要深入研究数字工具在工会组织中的实际应用情况。

Method: 通过17次定性访谈，分析各工会工作者如何使用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台进行劳工组织。

Result: 数字工具对当代劳工成功不可或缺，但同时也引入了技术安全、信息过载和建立信任与共识等新的复杂性。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，将数字组织与数字工具在工会中的角色联系起来，拓展了对数字组织的更广泛理解。

Abstract: The contemporary American labor force is highly dispersed, necessitating the use of digital communication tools to bridge spatial and temporal gaps in union organizing. This study provides an in-depth analysis of how workers within various labor unions utilize digital, text-based communication platforms -- including Discord, WhatsApp, and Slack -- for labor organizing. Through 17 qualitative interviews, we examine the challenges and opportunities presented by digital organizing, identifying both technical and social obstacles. Our findings reveal that although digital tools are integral to contemporary labor successes, they also introduce new complexities, such as navigating technical security, managing information overload, and building trust and consensus. Based on these insights, we draw connections to broader understandings of digital organizing and the role of digital tools in unions.

</details>


### [169] [DataMagic: Transforming Tabular Data into Data Insight Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20388)
*Yupeng Xie,Chen Ma,Zhenyang Wang,Liangwei Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Chuxuan Zeng,Zhouan Shen,Boyan Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DataMagic是一个端到端交互系统，能将原始表格数据和自然语言查询自动转化为叙述性数据洞察视频。它通过引入DVSpec声明式规范确保数据保真度，采用Generate-then-Orchestrate多智能体架构应对设计空间组合爆炸，并支持三种交互模式与基于来源的数据问答，将单向视频转化为可探索的交互式数据界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量数据视频的制作需要数据分析、叙事设计和视频制作等多领域专业知识，现有方法存在不足：静态可视化工具缺乏叙事逻辑和动画；创作工具需要用户预准备可视化而非从原始数据出发；像素级视频生成模型无法保证数据保真度和可溯源性。

Method: DataMagic系统包含三个核心创新：(1) 引入DVSpec声明式规范，通过数据驱动的语义引用将视觉和动画元素绑定到底层数据字段；(2) 采用Generate-then-Orchestrate多智能体架构，并行生成候选场景再通过全局编排优化叙事连贯性；(3) 利用DVSpec对逻辑和渲染的解耦，支持三种交互模式和基于结构化来源的数据问答。

Result: 在109个真实世界样本上的评估验证了DataMagic系统的有效性。

Conclusion: DataMagic提供了一个从原始数据到交互式数据视频的全流程解决方案，在保证数据保真度的同时，通过多智能体架构和声明式规范有效应对了设计空间爆炸的挑战，并将单向视频转变为可探索的交互式数据界面。

Abstract: Data videos integrate dynamic charts, voice narration, and synchronized animations to communicate data insights as temporal narratives, making them an effective medium for improving data consumption efficiency in the data management lifecycle. However, producing high-quality data videos requires expertise spanning data analysis, narrative design, and video production. Existing approaches fall short: static visualization tools (e.g., BI dashboards) lack narrative logic and animation; authoring tools require users to pre-prepare visualizations rather than working from raw data; pixel-level video generation models cannot guarantee data fidelity or provenance. We demonstrate DataMagic, an end-to-end interactive system that transforms raw tabular data and natural language queries into narrative data-insight videos. To ensure data fidelity, DataMagic introduces the declarative specification DVSpec, which binds visual and animation elements to underlying data fields through data-driven semantic references. To address the combinatorial explosion of the design space, DataMagic adopts a Generate-then-Orchestrate multi-agent architecture that generates candidate scenes in parallel and then optimizes narrative coherence through global orchestration. Leveraging DVSpec's decoupling of logic and rendering, the system further supports three interaction modes and structured provenance-based data Q&A, transforming one-way videos into explorable interactive data interfaces. Evaluation on 109 real-world samples validates the effectiveness of the DataMagic. Homepage: https://datamagic-home.github.io/

</details>


### [170] [Human-AI Agent Interaction in a Business Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18716)
*Kathrin Paimann,Elizangela Valarini,Sebastian Juhl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法（定性+定量）识别和评估了AI代理的积极用户体验（UX）原则与标准，探索了人机交互模式，并通过问卷实验评估了特定设计元素的效果，旨在促进AI代理在商业环境中的采用、信任建立和以用户为中心的决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理日益融入核心业务流程，理解和设计有效的人机交互模式对于创造价值至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的UX原则与评估方法。

Method: 采用混合方法：结合定性与定量技术探索人类与AI代理之间的交互模式，并以此为基础开发问卷实验，大规模评估特定设计元素的有效性。

Result: 识别了用户期望与需求，提出了促进采用、建立信任和支持用户中心决策的UX原则与标准，以及相应的测量方法。

Conclusion: 本基础性研究有助于在商业环境中开发更直观、更有效的人类-AI代理交互模式，为AI代理的UX设计提供了理论和实证依据。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly integrated into core business processes, understanding and designing effective interaction patterns between humans and AI agents becomes crucial for value creation. This study identifies and evaluates principles and criteria for a positive User Experience (UX) with AI agents, along with methods for its measurement. We identify user expectations and needs to facilitate adoption, build trust, and support user-centered decision-making by development teams. Using a mixed-methods approach that combines qualitative and quantitative techniques, we explore interaction patterns between humans and AI agents. The findings from this exploratory research serve as the basis to develop a survey experiment which evaluates the effectiveness of specific design elements on a larger scale. This foundational research contributes to the development of more intuitive and effective human-AI agent interactions in business settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“计算可识别性”概念，替代传统的理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索过程在指定误差容限内寻找经验估计量，从而在有限样本、模糊图条件、混合数据等实际场景中实现因果效应的可识别性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论可识别性依赖渐近性、无限数据等理想化条件，无法回答有限样本、模糊图标准则等实际识别问题，因此需要一种计算驱动的可识别性框架来填补理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”框架，定义有限计算搜索过程：在给定参数先验分布和搜索过程假设的条件下，通过搜索找到一个经验估计量，若该估计量在指定误差容限内满足要求，则认定可识别。通过多个实验验证该框架在不同场景下的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够处理有限样本下的可识别性、模糊图标准则下的可识别性、混合观测-干预数据的可识别性，以及反事实数据和估计量的可识别性等细粒度实际问题。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为因果推断提供了一种更贴近实际应用的识别判定方式，能够回答传统理论可识别性无法处理的实践性问题，是理论可识别性的重要补充。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [172] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GUARD框架，通过上下文路由器和不确定性门控温度机制，将多个分布偏移的时间序列基础模型知识蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现高精度科学预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在物理科学领域存在分布偏移和计算成本过高的问题，亟需一种能从失配的大模型中提取结构知识并训练轻量级专用预测器的方法，以实现边缘计算传感器网络中的高效部署。

Method: 提出Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (GUARD)框架，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器(Contextual Router)基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师模型；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制(Uncertainty-Gated Temperature)在教师置信度与领域现实偏离时自动衰减蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域，GUARD显著降低RMSE；在28.5%的最难实例上，域失配教师仍能优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 证明了域失配的基础模型仍可作为关键修正信号，实现了适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [173] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，在边缘端使用4-bit量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词后再传输至云端大语言模型。实验表明，在248个提示词语料库上，每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，响应质量在预设非劣效性范围内与原始路径相当，每次调用可节省70-270 uWh能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的预填充阶段正成为云级能耗的重要来源。用户提示中常包含社交脚手架语言（礼貌标记、道歉性开场白、重复及建立 rapport 的语言），这些对人类交流重要但对机器推理携带的信息边际价值低。这种差异被称为"社会-语义鸿沟"。本文旨在通过在边缘设备上压缩提示词来减少云LLM的输入token成本，从而降低能耗。

Method: 提出SPSD流水线：在边缘端使用4-bit量化（Q4_K_M）的Gemma-2-2B-Instruct作为小语言模型对用户提示进行情感保留语义蒸馏，压缩后传输至云端Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct进行评估。对安全关键领域采用基于规则的闸门保守路由至直通模式。评估使用248提示词语料库，通过盲审LLM-as-Judge评分（15分制，预设1分非劣效性边界）和余弦相似度进行质量评估。

Result: 每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用全部实现正向节省。LLM裁判评分中43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出。余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%的配对超过0.70参考阈值。每次调用净能量节省约70-270 uWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明，端侧提示蒸馏可以在实际非劣效性边界内减少云LLM输入token成本，同时保持响应质量，为降低LLM推理能耗提供了一种可行的边缘-云协同方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [174] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Med-DDPM三维医学扩散模型在三代NVIDIA架构上进行全面的性能分析，发现训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，存在内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率低下的问题。提出的TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局优化在A100上实现了SM cycles减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且不降低合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维MRI扩散模型（如Med-DDPM）因每样本需要数百次U-Net评估，导致GPU资源需求巨大，且内核行为高度异构，现有研究缺乏对其性能瓶颈的深入分析，因而需要系统性的性能剖析来指导优化。

Method: 在V100、A100、H100三代NVIDIA架构上对Med-DDPM进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和profiler优先级分数估计的全方位性能分析，并评估TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化。

Result: 训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，瓶颈源于内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率低。TF32和channels-last优化在A100上实现SM cycles减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且质量无损。

Conclusion: 架构感知的优化策略能显著提升三维医学扩散模型的GPU资源效率，在不牺牲合成质量的前提下大幅降低计算开销，为医疗AI模型的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [175] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的深层联系：ILL通过交替投影到分区格上学习信号的可解释规则，其中规则被解释为商变量的边际约束，而提升操作对应满足这些约束的最大无知重建（L2均匀性/最大熵），最终形成由学习到的抽象索引的对数线性因子图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将ILL学习到的概率规则赋予自然的概率图模型解释，阐明ILL与PGM、最大熵模型之间的关系。

Method: 通过形式化分析，将ILL中的分区视为确定性商变量，规则视为其边际律，规则集视为可解释抽象的边际约束集合；一般提升为满足约束的所有联合分布族，特殊提升采用基于L2均匀性（与最大熵密切相关）的最大无知重建；在Shannon熵提升下，相同约束产生由学习到的抽象索引的对数线性因子图。

Result: ILL应被理解为针对商变量的可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法，其格边编码的是抽象的细化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖，因此ILL不是贝叶斯网络。

Conclusion: 该视角阐明了ILL与图模型和最大熵模型的关系，并为推理、可辨识性和混合符号-概率学习指明了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [176] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于两参数Weibull框架诊断变压器权重分布，研究了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ(t)增长、过冲然后松弛的动力学机制。通过推导AdamW更新的三力分解（对齐力、注入力和衰减力），并在自训练Pythia-70M模型上验证了对齐力主导上升阶段（占绝对力预算88-94%），对齐与衰减接近平衡时解释过渡到松弛阶段。针对真实模型提出样条位移方法恢复对齐力（准确率约92-94%），并观察到λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练过程中权重分布尺度参数λ(t)为何先增长、过冲然后松弛的潜在动力学机制。

Method: 推导AdamW更新中平方权重的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；在自训练Pythia-70M模型上利用真实优化器动量验证；提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导上升阶段（88-94%预算），对齐与衰减平衡时过渡到松弛；样条位移方法恢复对齐力准确率约92-94%，约为朴素两基点线的两倍；RMS-to-Weibull重建偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: 三力分解框架有效解释了AdamW训练中权重尺度的动力学行为，样条位移方法使分析可扩展到真实模型，数据一致性对权重尺度的影响留作后续研究。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [177] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑盒优化领域，无需手工设计稀疏算子即可联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值，在Lunar Lander基准上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EDA无法处理稀疏参数空间（大部分系数为零）的黑盒优化问题；现有的稀疏黑盒优化器重新引入了手工设计的稀疏算子、双层优化方案、零阈值等假设，违背了EDA避免手工算子设计的初衷。作者旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯分布作为EDA的采样律；该分布通过潜高斯模型同时建模稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者间的交互，实现了无层级结构的联合优化；并设计了实用的基于摊销逆推的估计器来恢复潜相关结构。

Result: 潜参数在观测样本下可识别；估计器能准确恢复潜相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，且找到的控制器仅需少量活跃参数，优于稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和特设稀疏EDA。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA推广至稀疏黑盒优化，无需手工设计稀疏算子，实现了稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化，在性能上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [178] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合自对弈强化学习与少量人类演示数据的驾驶策略训练方法（SPICED），仅需30分钟人类数据（比模仿学习少2500倍）即可在单GPU上15小时训练出与人类驾驶行为协调的策略，避免了纯自对弈产生的异化驾驶习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略会产生与人类驾驶习惯不相容的"异化"行为，而现有通过奖励工程和域随机化的缓解方法脆弱且劳动密集；同时完全依赖人类演示的模仿学习需要海量数据，成本高昂。

Method: 将人类演示数据作为正则化目标，叠加在最小安全目标到达奖励之上，形成一个自对弈强化学习+少量人类数据正则化的混合训练框架。

Result: 仅使用30分钟人类演示数据（比模仿学习少2500倍），在单张消费级GPU上15小时完成训练，训练出的策略能够与未见过的（held-out）人类轨迹协调交互。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据（30分钟）即可有效引导自对弈强化学习策略向人类兼容的行为方向收敛，避免了纯自对弈的异化问题，同时大幅降低了数据需求。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [179] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
