Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销分层贝叶斯预测推理。通过将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，并训练Transformer在序列化的先验与目标任务上学习，该方法能在不同先验族间自适应预测，在匹配贝叶斯最优性能的同时实现数量级的加速，并在现实时空温度预测任务上验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯预测推理方法存在计算成本高或需要限制性建模假设的问题；先验数据拟合和上下文模型虽提供摊销替代方案，但与训练先验耦合紧密，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的显式机制，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架：将先验信息显式编码为上下文数据集的前缀，训练Transformer在序列化的先验任务和目标任务上进行学习，从而实现跨先验族的自适应预测推理。

Result: 在包括元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验的一系列难度递增的评估中，该方法在匹配贝叶斯最优预测器的同时，实现了数量级的计算加速，并在现实世界时空温度预测基准上展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了现有摊销贝叶斯推理方法对新先验适应性不足的问题，在保持预测质量的前提下大幅降低计算开销，为分层贝叶斯预测推理提供了一种鲁棒且高效的方案。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [290] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于可预测性的隐私"（privacy via predictability）框架，通过量化攻击者在观察算法输出后对敏感信息预测能力的增量来度量隐私泄露。该框架与差分隐私不可比，但可互补使用，为细粒度隐私控制提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私的 worst-case 性质导致隐私-精度权衡代价过高，缺乏对攻击者先验知识和特定敏感信息的细粒度刻画。

Method: 提出可预测性隐私框架，建模攻击者核心知识（被攻破的部分数据集）及查询族；利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析渐近可预测性；设计可预测性校准的输出扰动机制。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私一般不可比；在仅剩一个未泄露个体且所有二值查询均为敏感的极端情形下，可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了一种比差分隐私更细粒度的隐私度量，可与DP互补使用以实现更灵活的隐私保护。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [291] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准（multicalibration）中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，给出了一个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性（OI）、全能预测器（omnipredictors）和泛预测器（panpredictors），解决了多个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多校准问题中，此前所有达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的预测器都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度明显更差。随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要是一个明确的开放问题。

Method: 提出了一种新的多校准算法，能够输出确定性预测器并达到极小极大最优样本复杂度。然后将该算法推广到满足结果不可区分性（OI）的确定性预测器，适用于有限或有限覆盖的测试集合。

Result: 给出了一个极小极大最优的多校准算法，输出确定性预测器；推广后得到了满足OI的确定性预测器以及确定性全能预测器和泛预测器，均具有最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 随机化对于多校准的最优样本复杂度并非必要，确定性预测器同样可以达到最优复杂度。该结果解决了一系列开放问题。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [292] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma的推理透明性，发现其虽在连续潜空间中进行大量计算（初始不透明串行深度是自回归模型Gemma 4的28.6倍），但通过可解释的token瓶颈映射信息可将不透明串行深度降至Gemma 4的1.1倍。算法透明性方面，扩散模型所有token预测在每步去噪中均可改变，存在非时序推理、token/序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等新现象。可监控性方面，DiffusionGemma与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma在连续潜空间中进行大量计算是否导致其推理透明性降低，这是理解模型决策、缓解误用与未对齐以及调试意外模型行为的关键能力。

Method: 将透明性分解为变量透明性（理解中间计算状态快照）和算法透明性（通过快照重构推理过程）。通过可解释的token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间信息流以降低不透明串行深度，并通过一系列可解释性案例研究揭示扩散模型特有的推理现象。

Result: 变量透明性：通过token瓶颈将不透明串行深度从28.6倍Gemma 4降至1.1倍。算法透明性：发现非时序推理、token/序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等新现象。可监控性：与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型推理透明性在变量层面可通过方法有效提升，算法层面存在独特挑战和新现象，但可监控性不亚于自回归模型。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Physical Atari系统，通过Robotroller机械臂驱动Atari CX40+手柄、Atari Devbox渲染游戏画面，结合摄像头和计算机，构建了一个低成本（<1000美元）、鲁棒且可访问的物理世界强化学习研究平台。实验表明强化学习算法可直接在机器人上学习，且部署时的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能，强调了设备上自适应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究多在模拟环境中进行，缺乏能够在物理世界中可靠、可重复地研究强化学习算法的低成本、鲁棒且易访问的平台。

Method: 设计了Robotroller（通过轴承驱动Atari CX40+手柄的机械臂）和Atari Devbox（渲染ALE游戏帧和奖励信号的设备），配合摄像头和计算机构成Physical Atari系统。采用轴承传动减少磨损，高频伺服状态监控软件干预以限制应力，使用消费级3D打印和市售组件以降低成本。

Result: 系统成本低于1000美元，可连续数周无机械故障运行强化学习实验。验证了强化学习算法可直接在机器人上学习，发现学习和部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: Physical Atari是一个鲁棒且易访问的物理强化学习研究平台，强调了设备上自适应对于在机器人上获得强性能至关重要。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [294] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WorkBenchMark，一个基于LEGO Duplo的机器人装配基准测试，包含400个任务和四个复杂度等级，并提出了基于"装配即拆卸"规划管线的基线解决方案，在所有复杂度等级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配需要将底层操作与任务级符号推理在物理约束下相结合，而当前的端到端学习方法尚无法可靠解决这一问题，因此需要建立一个标准化的基准测试来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了包含400个任务、四个复杂度等级的基准测试；提出了基于"装配即拆卸"（Assembly-by-Disassembly）规划管线的基线解决方案，采用开放词汇感知。

Result: 所提出的基于规划的管线在所有四个复杂度等级上均优于现代的视觉-语言-动作方法。

Conclusion: 该基准测试、仿真环境和基线实现将公开发布，以支持更广泛的机器人装配社区。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [295] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对3D场景图（3DSG）领域进行了统一且批判性的综述，形式化了其定义，分析了建模选择、构建流水线、下游应用及评估策略，并指出了开放挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSG已成为空间AI的强大表示，但其领域碎片化严重：不同社区采用不同的公式、构建流程和评估协议，导致方法难以比较、共同假设难以识别、实际部署挑战难以评估。

Method: 首先在统一定义下形式化3DSG，分析节点/边属性、层次结构、动态场景表示、可供性扩展等建模选择；然后回顾从原始传感器观测构建3DSG的技术（术语、惯例、方法）；最后考察下游应用和评估策略（从内在图质量到任务级性能）。

Result: 提供了一个统一的3DSG形式化定义，梳理了现有公式的建模选择、构建技术和评估方法，并建立网站（https://3dscenegraphs.com/）组织扩展综述内容。

Conclusion: 3DSG领域需要统一的形式化和评估协议以促进方法比较和实际部署，未来方向包括动态场景表示、可供性扩展等挑战。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [296] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散策略的视觉伺服方法，使用归一化标签角坐标作为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度，并采用在线训练范式不断扩展训练数据多样性。仿真和实物实验分别达到近100%和93%的成功率，且该扩散模块可集成到现有视觉伺服网络中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于回归的视觉伺服方法存在两个主要问题：单步映射对噪声敏感导致轨迹抖动，以及分布偏移时误差累积。扩散策略通过预测动作序列保持时间一致性，并通过隐式数据增强提高鲁棒性，本文旨在将扩散策略的优势引入视觉伺服领域。

Method: 基于扩散策略，使用归一化的标签角图像坐标作为输入，通过条件去噪过程生成相机速度。采用在线训练范式，通过交互式经验收集持续扩展训练数据多样性，以克服静态数据集训练的泛化限制。

Result: 仿真实验成功率达近100%，实物实验成功率达93%。将所提扩散模块集成到现有视觉伺服网络后，均实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散策略具有广泛适用性，不仅能用于特定管道，还能增强多种视觉伺服系统的性能。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [297] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），让具身编码智能体通过自我指导的玩耍阶段持续学习技能，将成功的执行提炼为持久代码技能库，在后续下游任务中复用这些技能，在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上分别比CaP-Agent0提升20.6和17.0个百分点，且无需微调即可迁移到其他Code-as-Policy智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码的策略机器人系统虽能编写、执行、修正程序，但技能获取依赖显式指令，缺乏持续的自我指导学习能力。作者希望让机器人在下游任务到来前通过自主玩耍阶段持续学习可复用技能。

Method: 提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），在玩耍阶段自主提出新颖且可学习的探索任务，规划并执行机器人代码策略，验证中间进度，诊断失败，以细粒度步骤级反馈重试，并将成功执行提炼为持久代码技能库；测试时从冻结库中检索相关技能辅助新任务。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上分别比CaP-Agent0提升20.6和17.0个百分点；学习的技能可通过上下文检索直接插入其他推理时Code-as-Policy智能体，在RoboSuite和真实世界迁移中分别提升8.9和8.8个百分点，无需微调底层模型。

Conclusion: 自我指导的玩耍阶段能够有效学习可迁移的代码技能，显著提升下游任务性能，且学到的技能具有良好的跨智能体迁移性。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively. Moreover, the learned skills can be plugged into other inference-time Code-as-Policy agents by simply retrieving them into the context, improving RoboSuite and real-world transfer by 8.9 and 8.8 points, respectively, without finetuning the underlying model.

</details>


### [298] [Simulating Robotic Locomotion in Sand: Resistive Force Theory in an Open-Source Physics Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19504)
*Ryan Walker Brown,Laura K. Treers,Kathryn A. Daltorio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒电阻力理论（3D RFT），用于模拟机器人在沙地等颗粒介质上的行走。实验表明，该实现能够在20%误差范围内预测六足机器人的行走距离和脚部下陷深度，为机器人设计提供了有效的仿真工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电阻力理论（RFT）能够近似计算在沙地等颗粒介质中 locomotion 的地面反作用力，避免了模拟单个颗粒的高计算成本，但这些工具尚未集成到常用的3D机器人仿真物理引擎中。因此，作者希望探究将RFT近似与标准动力学计算相结合，是否能为自由行走的机器人提供稳定的仿真基础。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理仿真引擎中实现了3D颗粒电阻力理论（3D RFT），并通过多种场景验证了末端执行器形状、速度和负载对关键趋势的保持效果。使用12自由度的六足机器人在沙地实验中验证了行走距离和脚部下陷深度的预测精度。

Result: 仿真实现能够在20%的误差范围内预测六足机器人在沙地上的行走距离和脚部沉陷深度，验证了末端执行器形状、速度和负载等关键趋势的准确性。

Conclusion: 尽管RFT存在固有的近似性，但该开源工具有潜力帮助开发和改进穿越颗粒介质的机器人设计，为在复杂地形中行走的机器人提供有效的仿真平台。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Resistive Force Theory (RFT) enable approximation of ground reaction forces for locomotion in sand without the computational expense of modeling interactions with individual grains. However, these tools have been absent in 3D physics engines commonly used for robot simulation. We explore if resistive force approximations are sufficient, when integrated with standard dynamics calculations, to provide a stable substrate for a freely walking robot. To determine this, we implement 3D Granular Resistive Force Theory (3D RFT) in a physics simulation engine, MuJoCo. We verify simulations in multiple scenarios to demonstrate that key trends due to end effector shape, speed, and loading are preserved. Our implementation predicts walking distance and foot sinkage of a 12-Degree of Freedom hexapod robot within 20\% of experiments in sand. While RFT has inherent approximations, the open source tool described here has potential to help develop new and improved robot designs to traverse granular media substrates.

</details>


### [299] [Proprioceptive Invariant State Estimation for Humanoid Robots on Non-Inertial Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19512)
*Falak Mandali,Zijian He,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）方法，仅利用本体感知（足部IMU）即可实现在非惯性地面上作业的人形机器人实时状态估计，无需外部传感器或地面运动直接测量，在Digit机器人实验中实现了96%的收敛速度提升和80%的位置估计误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖外部传感器或假设地面静止，无法处理人形机器人在移动地面（如晃动、旋转地面）上的状态估计问题，需要一种纯本体感知的解决方案。

Method: 采用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波框架，利用足部IMU获取站立脚的运动学约束，构建右不变测量模型以在大初始不确定性下获得良好误差动态，并通过可观测性分析确定状态可观测条件。

Result: 在Digit机器人上，站立和蹲起实验中相比现有InEKF方法，收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%；单轴旋转地面行走实验中，初始误差高达1m时平均估计误差小于9cm。

Conclusion: 所提InEKF方法仅凭本体感知即可在非惯性地面上实现高精度、快收敛的人形机器人状态估计，有效解决了地面运动带来的非线性问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an invariant extended Kalman filtering (InEKF) approach for real-time state estimation of humanoid robots operating on non-inertial ground using only onboard proprioceptive sensing. The proposed approach estimates the robot's base position and velocity relative to the moving ground frame without requiring direct measurements of ground motion or externally mounted sensors. By exploiting kinematic constraints at the stance foot through foot-mounted IMUs, the filter accounts for ground-induced nonlinearities in the process and measurement models while remaining fully proprioceptive. The estimator is formulated to admit a right-invariant measurement model, enabling favorable error dynamics under large initial uncertainties. Observability analysis establishes conditions under which the robot's relative base position and velocity are observable with respect to the non-inertial ground frame. Experiments with the Digit humanoid robot standing and squatting atop a swaying and pitching ground showcase a 96% speedup in convergence rate and an 80% reduction in position estimate errors over existing InEKFs. Walking experiments on a uni-axially rotating ground achieve an average estimation error of less than 9 cm for an initial error of up to 1 m.

</details>


### [300] [A Categorial and Sheaf-Theoretic Semantics for Autonomic Component Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19525)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用范畴论和层论为SCEL（软件组件复合语言）构建多层数学模型，将机器人社会形式化为拓扑空间上的层，通过层的“粘合”操作模拟信息共享，并用层上同调量化系统故障，从而将分布式系统验证转化为数学对象的几何分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自治代理系统（如机器人集群和网络化物理系统）对传统形式化方法构成巨大挑战。SCEL虽然提供了此类系统的形式化模型，但其操作语义不适合推理全局性、结构性和涌现性属性，亟需新的数学框架来弥补这一不足。

Method: 使用范畴论和层论构建SCEL的多层数学模型：将组件视为拓扑空间中的点，组件集合视为开集，分布式知识作为层的数据；计算过程（如信息共享）通过层的“粘合”操作实现；系统故障被理解为拓扑障碍，通过层上同调进行量化度量。

Result: 提出了一种将分布式系统验证转化为数学对象几何分析的新范式，能够通过层上同调量化系统故障，为鲁棒自主系统的设计提供结构性洞察。

Conclusion: 该方法将复杂分布式系统的验证问题转化为数学对象的几何分析，提供了深层结构性的理解，有助于设计更鲁棒的自主系统。

Abstract: The proliferation of large-scale, decentralized systems of autonomous agents, such as swarms of robots and networked cyber-physical systems, presents a formidable challenge to traditional formal methods. The Software Component Ensemble Language (SCEL) offers a formal model for such systems, but its operational semantics is not ideal for reasoning about global, structural, and emergent properties. This report proposes a new, multi-layered mathematical model for SCEL using category theory and sheaf theory. We argue that a society of robots described in SCEL can be formally modeled as a sheaf on a topological space, where components are points, ensembles are open sets, and distributed knowledge forms the sheaf's data. In this framework, computational processes like information sharing become equivalent to the sheaf-theoretic operation of "gluing" local data. System failures can then be understood and quantified as topological obstructions, measurable by sheaf cohomology. This approach transforms the verification of a complex distributed system into the analysis of the geometry of a mathematical object, providing deep, structural insights for the design of robust autonomic systems.

</details>


### [301] [SCAN-Planner: Spatial Collision-Aware Local Planning for Route-Guided Long-Range Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19555)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yiwen Fu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAN-Planner 提出了一种空间碰撞感知的局部规划框架，利用偏航感知的双圆柱足迹模型和投影 A* 搜索，实现四足机器人在狭窄通道、杂乱室内和三维非结构化环境中的安全、平滑、高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部规划器通常采用各向同性的几何膨胀近似机器人，或依赖平面/高程地图表示，导致在狭窄空间中运动保守，且难以处理悬空结构的避障问题。

Method: 1) 提出偏航感知的双圆柱足迹模型，在膨胀的三维占据地图中通过稀疏查询实现全身碰撞评估；2) 引入投影 A* 搜索，在插值的地面跟随表面上生成无碰撞引导路径，并通过 z 梯度抑制实现水平避障与垂直稳定；3) 采用机器人中心滑动地图与边界回退机制，支持大规模部署中的高分辨率局部碰撞检测和死胡同恢复。

Result: 仿真与实物实验表明，SCAN-Planner 在密集杂乱场景、三维非结构化环境、楼梯穿越及长距离导航任务中均能生成安全、平滑且高效的轨迹。

Conclusion: SCAN-Planner 通过空间碰撞感知的局部规划框架，有效克服了传统方法在狭窄空间和复杂三维环境中的保守性问题，提升了四足机器人的全地形导航能力。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to navigate through narrow passages, cluttered indoor scenes, and large-scale 3D unstructured environments. Existing local planners commonly approximate the robot using isotropic geometric inflation or rely on planar and elevation-map representations, leading to conservative motion in tight spaces and limited reasoning about overhanging structures. This letter presents SCAN-Planner, a spatial collision-aware local planning framework for long-range quadruped navigation. A yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint is used to model the elongated robot body, enabling whole-body collision evaluation through sparse queries in an inflated 3D occupancy map. We further introduce a projected A* search that generates collision-free guidance on an interpolated ground-following surface, with z-gradient suppression to avoid obstacles horizontally while maintaining vertical stability. For large-scale deployment, a robot-centric sliding map with boundary fallback provides high-resolution local collision checking and recovery from local dead ends. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCAN-Planner generates safe, smooth, and efficient trajectories in dense clutter, 3D unstructured scenes, stair traversal, and long-range navigation tasks.

</details>


### [302] [pdSTL: Probabilistic Differentiable Signal Temporal Logic for Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19561)
*Bennett Dogbey,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出pdSTL框架，将概率语义与可微鲁棒性结合，用于具有随机动力学和感知噪声的自主机器人轨迹优化，在仿真和真实四旋翼飞行实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL扩展要么缺乏可微性，要么忽略信念空间不确定性，无法同时满足梯度优化需求和概率安全保证。

Method: 提出pdSTL，采用区间值概率语义计算保守满足边界，通过STL语法树组合传播；将时序鲁棒性评估建模为LSTM风格循环展开，实现线性时间可微监控。

Result: 在仿真避障、变道和真实Crazyflie四旋翼飞行实验中，pdSTL在维持安全裕度方面显著优于确定性可微STL。

Conclusion: pdSTL能够在真实不确定性下实现高效优化并提供形式化概率保证，适用于端到端轨迹优化。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in uncertain environments must satisfy complex temporal and safety specifications despite stochastic dynamics and sensing noise. While Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers robustness measures for gradient-based optimization, existing extensions either lack differentiability or ignore belief-space uncertainty. We introduce pdSTL (probabilistic differentiable Signal Temporal Logic), a framework that unifies probabilistic semantics with differentiable robustness over belief trajectories. pdSTL employs interval-valued probabilistic semantics to compute conservative satisfaction bounds, propagated compositionally through the STL syntax tree. We formulate the temporal robustness evaluation as a recurrent, LSTM-style unfolding of STL operators, enabling linear-time, differentiable monitoring suitable for end-to-end trajectory optimization. We validate pdSTL on simulated obstacle avoidance, lane-change maneuvers, and real-world Crazyflie quadcopter flight experiments under aerodynamic disturbances. Results demonstrate that pdSTL achieves efficient optimization with formal probabilistic guarantees, significantly outperforming deterministic differentiable STL in maintaining safety margins under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [303] [One Demo is Worth a Thousand Trajectories: Action-View Augmentation for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19586)
*Chuer Pan,Litian Liang,Dominik Bauer,Eric Cousineau,Benjamin Burchfiel,Siyuan Feng,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据增强框架，利用鱼眼高斯泼溅和轨迹优化，从少量真实数据生成视觉逼真的鱼眼图像序列和物理可行的动作轨迹，提升操控策略在场景变化和障碍物下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略对机器人初始配置和未见障碍物敏感，微小变化会导致观测分布外推和灾难性执行失败，而大规模数据收集成本高昂。

Method: 1) 提出适配广角鱼眼相机的新型高斯泼溅公式，用于3D场景重建与编辑（加入未见物体）；2) 利用轨迹优化生成平滑、无碰撞、利于视图渲染的动作轨迹；3) 从新颖视角渲染视觉观测，生成鱼眼图像序列及对应动作轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该增强框架在相同场景和含障碍物的增强场景中均显著提升了多种操控任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效缓解分布外观测问题，提升操控策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性，减少对大规模数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for manipulation have demonstrated remarkable potential in modeling complex robotic behaviors, yet minor alterations in the robot's initial configuration and unseen obstacles easily lead to out-of-distribution observations. Without extensive data collection effort, these result in catastrophic execution failures. In this work, we introduce an effective data augmentation framework that generates visually realistic fisheye image sequences and corresponding physically feasible action trajectories from real-world eye-in-hand demonstrations, captured with a portable parallel gripper with a single fisheye camera. We introduce a novel Gaussian Splatting formulation, adapted to wide FoV fisheye cameras, to reconstruct and edit the 3D scene with unseen objects. We utilize trajectory optimization to generate smooth, collision-free, view-rendering-friendly action trajectories and render visual observations from corresponding novel views. Comprehensive experiments in simulation and the real world show that our augmentation framework improves the success rate for various manipulation tasks in both the same scene and the augmented scene with obstacles requiring collision avoidance.

</details>


### [304] [Safe, Real-Time Active Model Discrimination and Fault Diagnosis for Nonlinear Systems via Differentiable Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19590)
*Xinpei Ni,Melkior Ornik,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向含过程/测量扰动的连续时间非线性系统的安全、实时主动故障诊断与模型辨识算法。该方法通过输出反馈时变策略优化，在有限时域内鲁棒地满足状态-输入安全约束，并驱动系统产生与至多一个候选模型一致的采样测量值，实现确定性诊断。利用区间过近似可达集和可微诊断目标函数，结合JAX和可微可达性原语在线高效求解。在多种高维非线性机器人系统（含11种故障模式）上验证，诊断时间低于50ms，成功率和速度均优于基线，并提供形式化安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动故障诊断方法在处理不确定连续时间非线性系统时，难以同时保证实时性、安全约束满足以及多模型间的确定性辨识，亟需一种可在线求解、安全有保证的框架。

Method: 将故障诊断表述为输出反馈时变策略优化问题，采用区间过近似方法计算可达集，构造可微诊断目标函数（最小化候选模型间可达输出集的重叠），利用JAX和可微可达性原语实现梯度优化在线求解。

Result: 在四旋翼、战斗机模型、差动驱动机器人、四足导航等多系统上验证，支持多达11种故障模式，诊断时间<50ms，在辨识成功率和速度上均优于基线方法，且具备形式化安全保证。

Conclusion: 提出了一种兼顾安全性和实时性的主动故障诊断方法，通过可微优化和区间可达性分析实现了高效的确定性模型辨识，适用于含扰动的非线性系统。

Abstract: We present a safe, real-time algorithm for active fault diagnosis and model discrimination for uncertain continuous-time nonlinear systems with process and measurement disturbances. Given a finite set of candidate models representing nominal and faulty modes, including actuator and sensor faults, we formulate an output-feedback, time-varying policy optimization problem that (i) robustly enforces state-input safety constraints over a finite horizon and (ii) drives the system to produce sampled measurements consistent with at most one model, enabling deterministic diagnosis. To solve this problem in real time, we develop a tractable approximation using interval over-approximations of reachable state and output sets, and encode diagnosability via a differentiable objective that penalizes overlap between the reachable output sets of possible models. The resulting optimization is solved efficiently online with gradient-based methods using JAX and differentiable reachability primitives. We evaluate our method on sensor and actuator fault diagnosis (up to 11 fault modes) in several high-dimensional nonlinear robotic systems, including a simulated quadrotor and fighter-jet model, a hardware differential-drive robot, and quadrupedal navigation. Across these case studies, our approach achieves reliable model discrimination in under 50 ms, outperforming baselines in discrimination success rate and speed while providing formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [305] [Fail-RAG : A Retrieval Augmented Generation Informed Framework for Robot Failure Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19598)
*Ameya Salvi,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Fail-RAG，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的机器人操作故障检测框架，通过将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入并与故障数据库进行相似性查询，结合视觉语言模型（VLM）分析故障。在仓库自动化场景的仿真和物理实验中，Fail-RAG在五种机器人操作类型的故障检测准确率上平均比直接使用VLM高出25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库自动化中机器人需要应对动态环境中的意外事件（故障），而基于规则的传统检测方法难以适应环境和任务动态变化导致的故障形态变化，亟需更灵活、鲁棒的故障检测方法。

Method: 提出Fail-RAG框架：将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入向量并与故障数据库进行相似度检索，检索到的相关故障案例结合视觉语言模型（VLM）按照指令模板对当前故障进行分析和详细描述。

Result: 在固定机械臂和移动机械手进行的仿真与物理实验中，针对仓库自动化常见的五种机器人操作任务，Fail-RAG的故障检测准确率平均比直接使用现成VLM高出25个百分点。

Conclusion: Fail-RAG通过RAG机制为VLM提供相关故障上下文信息，显著提升了动态环境中机器人故障检测的准确性和实用性，验证了其在真实世界仓库自动化场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Industry automation is witnessing an evolution in robotics driven by both technological breakthroughs and societal changes: progress towards generalist robots, embodied and physical artificial intelligence (AI), and increasing labor shortage in manufacturing.An intelligent autonomous robot needs to not only act according to planned motions but also react to any unexpected events. In this study, we focus on such unexpected events in warehouses where robots are used for material handling. Specifically, we refer to any unexpected events as failures and develop methods to detect robot operations related failures. Rule-based detection methods may break since the form of failures could change due to the dynamic nature of both environments and tasks. We propose 'Fail-RAG', a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)-based failure detection framework where failure images and context information are embedded and queried against a failure database by calculating their similarities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are further used to analyze failures and provide details by following our instruction template. We evaluated the performance of Fail-RAG by conducting both simulation and physical experiments using fixed robot arms and a mobile manipulator for multiple tasks that are common in warehouse automation. Fail-RAG achieved 25 percentage point higher failure detection accuracy on average across five types of robot operations compared to using off-the-shelf VLMs, indicating its effectiveness for real-world failure detection.

</details>


### [306] [Formal Verification of Learned Multi-Agent Communication Policies via Decision Tree Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19632)
*Ahmad Farooq,Kamran Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向多智能体强化学习通信策略的端到端安全验证框架，通过策略抽象（决策树蒸馏）将神经策略转化为可解释模型，结合PRISM概率模型检验和PCTL属性验证，在5-7智能体无人机编队场景中实现88.9%属性满足率，且验证的安全属性可迁移回原始网络（偏差≤0.6百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在无人机群和自动驾驶车队等安全关键领域缺乏形式化安全保证，现有神经策略无法提供可验证的部署保障。

Method: 四阶段流水线：1）领域特定特征提取；2）决策树蒸馏（97.9% fidelity）；3）自动翻译为PRISM概率模型检验规范；4）基于PCTL属性的组合验证（成对分解+联合边界聚合+经验邻居建模）。

Result: VQ-VIB策略在5-7智能体无人机编队中验证18个时序逻辑属性，88.9%满足率，5个安全阈值全部达标（碰撞概率0.3% vs 1%阈值）。蒙特卡罗验证显示安全属性迁移偏差≤0.6百分点。离散VQ-VIB消息相比连续方法fidelity提升11.6-13.6百分点，验证速度加快3-4倍。

Conclusion: 该框架为深度MARL与形式化安全工作流之间提供了实用桥梁，通过策略抽象实现了可验证的安全保证，支撑多机器人安全部署。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables agents to develop coordination strategies through emergent communication, but neural policies lack the formal safety guarantees required for safety-critical robotic deployment in drone swarms and autonomous vehicle fleets. We present the first end-to-end framework for safety verification of learned multi-agent communication policies through policy abstraction: neural policies are distilled into interpretable decision trees, then formally verified, with empirical validation confirming that verified safety properties transfer to original networks. Our four-stage pipeline consists of domain-specific feature extraction from agent observations, decision tree distillation achieving 97.9% +/- 1.2% fidelity to neural policies, automated translation to PRISM probabilistic model checker specifications with complete feature-to-state-variable correspondence, and compositional verification of Probabilistic Computation Tree Logic (PCTL) properties via pairwise decomposition with union-bound aggregation and empirical neighbor modeling. Evaluating Vector-Quantized Variational Information Bottleneck (VQ-VIB) policies for multi-drone coordination with 5-7 agents, we verify 18 temporal logic properties across safety, liveness, and cooperation, achieving 88.9% property satisfaction with all five safety thresholds satisfied (0.3% collision probability vs. 1% threshold). Monte Carlo validation of original neural policies confirms that verified safety properties transfer with <=0.6 percentage-point deviation (95% CI). Discrete VQ-VIB messages provide +11.6 to +13.6 percentage-point fidelity advantages over continuous methods, enabling 3-4x faster verification. Our framework provides empirically validated safety verification for distilled policy abstractions, serving as a practical bridge between deep MARL and formal safety workflows for multi-robot deployment.

</details>


### [307] [CTS-MoE: Implicit Terrain Adaptation via Mixture-of-Experts for Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19633)
*Francisco Affonso,Matheus P. Angarola,Ana Luiza Mineiro,Aditya Potnis,Marcelo Becker,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CTS-MoE框架，通过密集混合专家网络与多评论家架构，解决四足机器人跨越不连续地形的多任务强化学习中的行为共享与价值干扰矛盾，在仿真和真实场景中优于单策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不连续地形（楼梯、间隙、障碍物）上的感知四足运动需要自适应行为，单一保守步态无法应对突变的拓扑结构。多任务强化学习中存在共享与分离的张力——任务共享运动基础但奖励冲突，现有单策略牺牲专化能力，层次化子策略牺牲泛化能力。

Method: 提出CTS-MoE：1) 密集混合专家（MoE）actor + 基于感知的门控机制组合共享行为；2) 多评论家架构+任务特定价值头防止干扰；3) 端到端单阶段师生并发训练处理部分可观测性，避免顺序蒸馏；4) 部署时仅依赖感知进行路由，无需高层选择器或地形分类器。

Result: 在Unitree Go1仿真与实物实验中，CTS-MoE在已见和未见地形上均展现任务感知专化能力，相比单策略基线，跟踪误差更低、成功率更高。

Conclusion: CTS-MoE通过密集MoE与多评论家架构有效平衡了多任务四足运动中的共享与分离需求，感知驱动的门控机制实现了跨地形自适应的鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion over discontinuous terrain (e.g., stairs, gaps, and obstacles) requires adaptive behavior, as a single conservative gait cannot produce the anticipatory maneuvers needed for abrupt topology changes. Cast as multi-task reinforcement learning, this problem introduces a tension between sharing and separation. Tasks use a common locomotion base but have conflicting rewards, so a policy must share behavior while avoiding value interference. Prior work addresses only one side, with monolithic policies sacrificing specialization and hierarchical sub-policies sacrificing generalization across transitions and unseen terrain. We propose CTS-MoE, which combines a dense mixture-of-experts actor with perception-based gating to compose shared behaviors and a multi-critic with task-specific value heads to prevent interference. The model is trained end-to-end in a single-stage concurrent teacher-student setup that handles partial observability and avoids sequential distillation, with task labels used only during training. At deployment, routing depends solely on perception, allowing terrain adaptation without a high-level selector or terrain classifier. Experiments on a Unitree Go1 in simulation and on hardware across seen and unseen terrains show task-aware specialization, with lower tracking error and higher success rates than monolithic baselines. Project Website: https://cts-moe.github.io/ .

</details>


### [308] [Scaling Self-Play for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19641)
*Luke Rowe,Roger Girgis,Rodrigue de Schaetzen,Daphne Cornelisse,Alaap Grandhi,Felix Heide,Eugene Vinitsky,Christopher Pal,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Gigapixel，一个高吞吐量批处理驾驶模拟器，通过自对弈DAgger训练和轻量级感知适配，使基于像素的端到端驾驶模型能够通过大规模自对弈训练，在无需人类轨迹监督的情况下在HUGSIM和NAVSIM-v2基准上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型依赖离线人类演示数据训练，状态覆盖有限且缺乏闭环反馈，导致部署时易出现复合误差并在长尾交互场景中表现脆弱。本文旨在通过大规模自对弈训练克服这些局限性。

Method: 1) Gigapixel模拟器：高吞吐量批处理驾驶模拟器，采用透视渲染简化边界框世界，保持关键场景结构，实现50K agent步/秒的吞吐量；2) 自对弈DAgger训练：通过在线策略蒸馏从特权RL教师训练基于像素的策略；3) 轻量级感知适配：将自对弈训练的策略迁移到真实传感器数据。

Result: 在HUGSIM和NAVSIM-v2基准上取得竞争性能，且自对弈训练的规模与策略性能呈正比关系。

Conclusion: 自对弈是一种实用且可扩展的端到端驾驶模型训练策略，能在无需人类轨迹监督的情况下实现高效训练，规模扩展带来性能增益。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on offline human-demonstration datasets that provide limited state coverage and often no closed-loop feedback, making them prone to compounding errors when deployed in closed-loop and brittle to long-tail agent interactions. To overcome these limitations, we propose an alternative strategy for training end-to-end driving models: large-scale self-play directly from pixels in simulation. While prior self-play approaches have shown promising transfer to real-world driving, they typically assume vectorized Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) observations that are incompatible with end-to-end policies operating directly on sensor observations. To this end, we introduce Gigapixel, a high-throughput batched driving simulator with perspective rendering, enabling scalable self-play directly from pixel observations. Rather than targeting compute-costly photorealistic sensor simulation, Gigapixel renders a simplified bounding-box world that preserves essential scene structure while achieving throughput at 50k agent steps per second. Since direct pixel-space self-play RL is prohibitively sample-inefficient at end-to-end model scale, we propose self-play DAgger training: we train pixel-based policies in self-play via on-policy distillation from a privileged RL teacher. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we subsequently transfer the self-play trained policies to real-world sensor data through lightweight perception adaptation. Policies trained in Gigapixel and adapted to real-world sensor data achieve competitive performance on the HUGSIM and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks without human trajectory supervision. Moreover, scaling self-play training yields proportional gains in policy performance, establishing self-play as a practical and scalable strategy for training end-to-end models.

</details>


### [309] [DF-ExpEnse: Diffusion Filtered Exploration for Sample Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19656)
*Calvin Luo,Chen Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DF-ExpEnse探索技术，通过生成式控制策略的多模态建模和评论家集成，提升在线经验收集质量，从而在微调预训练生成式控制策略时提高样本效率，并在多智能体场景下支持协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能机器人决策制定的自然方案是从预训练的生成式控制策略（已总结离线经验）初始化，并使其适应自收集的在线经验。现有方法在在线经验收集的质量上存在不足，需要一种能提升在线经验收集质量从而提高微调样本效率的探索技术。

Method: 1) 利用生成式控制策略的多模态建模能力构建表达力强且可评估的候选动作集；2) 采用评论家集成（ensemble of critics）识别在质量与探索兴趣之间取得最佳平衡的动作；3) 在群体设置中进一步实现跨智能体通信以促进协同探索。

Result: 在多种操作和 locomotion 任务上，与默认微调和替代动作选择方案相比，DF-ExpEnse 一致地带来了样本效率提升。

Conclusion: DF-ExpEnse 可无缝集成现有的通过强化学习微调预训练生成式控制策略的方法，并有效提升样本效率。

Abstract: A natural recipe for intelligent robotic decision-making is initializing from pretrained generative control policies, which have summarized offline experience, and adapting them to self-collected online experience. We present DF-ExpEnse, an exploration technique that improves the quality of online experience collection, thus increasing finetuning sample-efficiency. DF-ExpEnse leverages the multimodal modeling capabilities of the generative control policy to create an expressive and tractably evaluatable candidate set. It then utilizes an ensemble of critics to identify the action that best balances quality with high exploration interest. In fleet settings, DF-ExpEnse further enables cross-agent communication to facilitate collaborative exploration as a group. DF-ExpEnse can be seamlessly integrated with existing strategies that finetune pretrained generative control policies via reinforcement learning. We experimentally validate consistent sample-efficiency benefits through DF-ExpEnse across a variety of manipulation and locomotion tasks, compared to default finetuning and alternative action selection schemes. Project can be found at https://df-expense.github.io.

</details>


### [310] [Safe Local Navigation for Ackermann-Steered Robots in Unmapped Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19672)
*Christian Schaible,Shahin Sirouspour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于阿克曼转向移动机器人在无全局目标的无地图环境中的安全局部导航控制框架。通过检测局部障碍物确定最安全航向角，构建左右边界线实现障碍物分离，利用凸二次优化最大化车辆与障碍物的间距，并通过反馈线性化控制器跟踪局部参考路径以保证安全。实验表明，该方法相比现有探索规划器，导航路径更安全且计算时间显著缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法在无全局目标的无地图环境中缺乏高效且安全的局部导航方案，探索型规划器计算时间长且路径安全性不足。

Method: 1) 基于局部障碍物检测确定最大开阔空间方向作为最安全航向角；2) 在车辆左右两侧构建边界线（通过凸二次优化最大化车辆-障碍物间距）；3) 可选条件保持边界线平行性并平滑突变；4) 使用反馈线性化控制器调节车辆与边界线的距离，实现局部参考路径跟踪。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法比现有探索型规划器产生更安全的导航路径，且计算时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 提出的控制框架能够有效实现无地图环境下阿克曼转向机器人的安全局部导航，兼具高安全性和低计算开销，且提供了开源代码便于应用。

Abstract: A control framework is proposed for safe local navigation of mobile robots equipped with Ackermann steering in unmapped environments where a global goal is absent. Based on local obstacle detections, the safest heading angle is determined along the direction of the largest open space ahead of the vehicle. Guided by this direction, bounding lines are constructed on the left and right sides of the vehicle to achieve obstacle separation. These bounding lines are obtained by solving a convex quadratic optimization that maximizes vehicle-to-obstacle clearance. Optionally, conditions are imposed on the bounding lines to preserve parallelism and smooth abrupt changes from prior control steps. A feedback-linearizing controller is then used to regulate the vehicle's distance from one or both bounding lines, effectively enabling tracking of a local reference path that preserves safety through obstacle clearance maximization. Open-source code is included for the application of this control scheme. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method produces safer navigation paths with significantly shorter computation times, compared to some existing exploration-based planners.

</details>


### [311] [ForEnt: A Multi-Modal Dataset for Characterizing Quadruped Robot Entrapments in Forest Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19675)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ForEnt，一个多模态数据集，用于研究四足机器人在森林环境中被植被缠绕（entrapment）导致的失效模式。数据集使用Unitree Go2四足机器人在英国南安普顿Common Woodlands的8个森林地点采集，包含1.7公里遍历、11个序列、69次缠绕事件，提供RGB-D图像、LiDAR扫描、本体感知数据和第三人称视频等时间同步的传感器数据，旨在降低开发鲁棒四足机器人在复杂森林环境中部署的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境中四足机器人常因腿部被藤蔓等植被缠绕而失去稳定性并倾倒，导致任务中断、需要人工干预，甚至损坏硬件。然而目前缺乏专门用于研究这类森林失效模式的公开数据集，因此作者构建了ForEnt数据集以填补这一空白。

Method: 使用低成本Unitree Go2四足机器人在英国南安普顿Common Woodlands的8个森林地点进行数据采集，共计约1.7公里遍历、11个序列，记录了69次缠绕事件。数据集包含时间同步的RGB-D图像、LiDAR扫描、本体感知数据和第三人称视频等多模态传感器数据，并提供了标记的传感器流以支持可重复的基准测试。

Result: 成功采集了包含69次缠绕事件的多模态数据集，覆盖8个森林地点的11个遍历序列，总里程约1.7公里。数据集实现了多种传感器的精确时间同步，并提供了标准化的标签用于缠绕检测算法的评估。

Conclusion: ForEnt数据集为研究森林环境中四足机器人的缠绕失效模式提供了标准化基准，支持缠绕检测策略的评估，从而降低了开发能够在复杂森林环境中鲁棒运行的四足机器人系统的门槛。

Abstract: Legged robots are increasingly deployed in forests for ecological surveying and monitoring, yet their autonomy is often interrupted consequent to the challenges posed in traversing forest environments. Forest entrapments, for example, when a robot's legs are ensnared in vines or other vegetation, result in loss of stability and toppling. Such events not only disrupt the mission and require manual intervention, but also risk damage to the robot hardware. To address the absence of a dedicated dataset to investigate these failure modes in forest environments, we present ForEnt, a multi-modal dataset collected with the low-cost Unitree Go2 quadruped across eight forest sites in the Southampton Common Woodlands, UK. For our dataset, over approximately 1.7 km of traversals in 11 sequences were conducted, yielding 69 recorded entrapment events. ForEnt includes time-synchronized RGB-D images, LiDAR scans, proprioceptive data, and third-person video, enabling analysis of terrain factors contributing to entrapment and providing labeled sensor streams for reproducible benchmarking. By supporting the evaluation of entrapment detection strategies, ForEnt lowers the barrier to developing robust quadruped robot deployments in challenging forest environments.

</details>


### [312] [Route-Constrained Robust Fusion Estimation for MEMS/GNSS Integrated Navigation of Unmanned Ground Vehicles in GNSS Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19687)
*Jingzhi Cui,Chao Zhang,Yuliang Mao,Shaolin Lü,Dongmei Li,Huan Che,Rong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对卫星信号遮挡下无人地面车辆在结构化道路环境中的累积定位漂移问题，本文提出了一种鲁棒的路径约束状态估计方法。该方法通过将历史航位推算轨迹与高清地图中的任务路段进行匹配，估计路径参考位置并作为伪位置观测融入扩展卡尔曼滤波，从而持续注入道路级路径约束，抑制相对于任务路线的位置偏差并间接改善方位估计。在长隧道、多段隧道和弯曲隧道三个场景下的实验表明，该方法能有效抑制卫星中断期间的误差累积，降低最大偏差风险，提升定位连续性和道路级可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人地面车辆在结构化道路环境中，因全球导航卫星系统信号严重遮挡导致累积定位漂移的问题。

Method: 建立历史航位推算轨迹与高清地图中任务路段之间的对应关系，通过二维刚体变换估计路径参考位置，将其作为伪位置观测融入扩展卡尔曼滤波框架。同时引入触发控制、匹配质量验证、路径偏移补偿和单次更新修正限制等工程策略。

Result: 在长隧道、多段隧道和弯曲隧道三个代表性场景中的实验表明，该方法能有效抑制卫星中断期间的误差累积，降低最大偏差风险，提升定位连续性和道路级可用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的路径约束状态估计方法能够有效抑制卫星信号遮挡下的定位漂移，改善无人地面车辆在结构化道路环境中的定位鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: To address cumulative localization drift of unmanned ground vehicles in structured road environments under severe Global Navigation Satellite System signal occlusion, this paper proposes a robust route-constrained state estimation method. During periods without satellite signals, the proposed method establishes the correspondence between the historical dead reckoning trajectory and local segments of the mission route extracted from a high-definition map, and estimates a route-referenced position via a two-dimensional rigid transformation. The estimated position is then formulated as a pseudo-position observation and incorporated into an Extended Kalman Filter update. In this way, route constraints at the road level can be continuously injected into a unified state estimation framework, thereby suppressing position deviation relative to the mission route while indirectly improving azimuth estimation. To enhance practical applicability, engineering strategies, such as trigger control, matching quality validation, route offset compensation, and single update correction limiting, are further introduced. Experiments in three representative scenarios, including a long tunnel, a multi-segment tunnel, and a curved tunnel, show that the proposed method effectively suppresses error accumulation during satellite outages, reduces the risk of large maximum deviation, and improves localization continuity and road-level usability.

</details>


### [313] [Comparative Study on Agility, Efficiency, and Impact Absorption of Bipedal Robots with Active Toes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19699)
*Joong-Gil Kim,Wontae Ye,Geunwoo Cho,Seong-Ho Yun,Se-Hyoung Cho,Yong-Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有14自由度主动脚趾的仿人双足机器人，通过高保真仿真训练环境，验证了主动脚趾在步行效率、冲击吸收和敏捷性方面的优势：相比无脚趾配置，有脚趾机器人在1.33m/s行走时能量消耗降低17.5%，脚跟冲击力减少5.0%，路径偏差分别降低25.0%（平均）和34.0%（最大）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类脚趾在行走中具有高效、敏捷和减震的关键作用，但现有机器人未能充分复现人类脚趾特性，也缺乏对主动脚趾效益的严格量化验证。

Method: 设计14自由度双足机器人模仿人类脚趾的轻量、高扭矩、鲁棒特性；构建考虑实际执行器、耦合传动和精确功耗的高保真仿真训练环境；采用最小化RL奖励函数，对有无主动脚趾两种配置进行相同训练流程的比较。

Result: 在1.33m/s步行速度下，有脚趾配置相比无脚趾配置：CoT降低17.5%，脚跟冲击力（GRF）减少5.0%；平均路径偏差降低25.0%，最大路径偏差降低34.0%。

Conclusion: 主动脚趾能够显著提升双足机器人的行走效率、冲击吸收能力和敏捷性，验证了仿人脚趾在机器人中的有效性。

Abstract: Human legs exhibit high efficiency, agility, and impact absorption, with toes playing a crucial role in these capabilities. While many attempts have been made to implement human-like toes in robots, they have not fully replicated human characteristics nor rigorously validated their benefits. We propose a 14-DOF biped robot emulating human toes' lightweight, high-torque, robust nature. To quantitatively analyze the effectiveness of the active toes in terms of agility, efficiency, and impact absorption, we developed a high-fidelity simulation training environment that reflects actual actuators with coupled transmissions and accurate power consumption. To ensure a fair comparison between configurations with and without active toes, we designed a minimal RL reward function and applied an identical training procedure to both. The simulation results indicate that, at 1.33 m/s walking, the toe-equipped robot reduced CoT by 17.5% and heel-strike GRF by 5.0% compared with the toe-ablation configuration. On the agility test, average and maximum path deviation decreased by 25.0% and 34.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [314] [A Differentiable Composite Approximation Framework for Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Maneuvering Modeling from Sea-Trial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19711)
*Aobo Wang,Aifei Xia,Zihao Wang,Lizhu Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微组合逼近方法，将多项式基函数与数据自适应基函数（神经网络）联合校准，并结合转弯运动海流估计补偿机制，用于基于实测数据的AUV机动建模，在海试数据上展示了优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线方法的轨迹与速度递归预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从基函数视角出发，传统机动模型使用预定义多项式基，数据驱动模型使用数据自适应基，两者各有局限。本文动机在于将两类基函数统一为可微组合逼近形式，实现联合校准，以兼顾物理先验的稳定性与数据驱动的适应性。

Method: 提出可微组合逼近框架：将多项式基成分与神经网络数据自适应基成分作为单一预测器的可微部分联合校准。采用基于梯度的共校准方法，包含灵敏度感知机制用于调控有界多项式更新，以及神经残差捕获剩余非线性差异。针对现场数据中海流影响，设计转弯运动海流估计与补偿程序，构建海流补偿学习目标用于训练和递推预测。

Result: 基于7米长AUV多机动工况海试数据评估，所提方法在递归轨迹和速度预测上优于纯多项式、纯神经网络和冻结先验混合基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了所提可微组合逼近框架在基于实测数据的AUV机动建模中的有效性和适用性，为融合物理先验与数据驱动方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Field-based modeling from onboard measurements can produce autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) maneuvering models that reflect real operating characteristics. From an approximation perspective, conventional maneuvering models use predefined constraint polynomial bases, whereas data-driven models use data-adaptive bases. Motivated by this basis-function view, this paper presents a differentiable composite-approximation formulation, in which the polynomial-basis component and the data-adaptive basis component are treated as differentiable parts of a single predictor and calibrated jointly. A gradient-based co-calibration method is developed for full-scale AUV maneuvering prediction, where a sensitivity-aware mechanism regulates bounded polynomial updates while the neural residual captures remaining nonlinear discrepancies under a shared prediction objective. To account for ocean-current effects in field data, a turning-motion-based current estimation and compensation procedure is incorporated to construct current-compensated learning targets for training and rollout. The framework is evaluated using sea-trial data collected from a 7-meter AUV under multiple maneuvering conditions. Results show that the proposed method improves recursive trajectory and velocity prediction compared with polynomial-only, neural-only, and frozen-prior hybrid baselines, demonstrating its applicability to field-data-based AUV maneuvering modeling.

</details>


### [315] [Bidirectional Tutoring for Developmental Motor Learning in Robots: Co-Developed Interaction Dynamics Support Stable Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19728)
*Rui Fukushima,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了双向互动教学对机器人运动技能发展的影响。通过在真实人形机器人上进行的物体操作实验（包括与人类互动和AI导师控制两种条件），发现双向教学相比单向教学能促进机器人形成更一致的行为模式、实现阶段性泛化，并逐渐减少对导师的依赖。研究基于自由能原理的神经网络和生成回放机制，验证了双向互动作为具身社会性学习框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人运动技能学习通常将教学视为单向过程（机器人被动接收示范），忽视了社会互动中导师和学习者动态双向适应的特性。作者假设双向教学能利用机器人已有经验作为先验约束，形成一致的行为模式以支持泛化，而单向教学则缺乏这种约束导致行为模式更分散。

Method: 使用物理人形机器人进行物体操作任务，设计了两种实验条件：1）真实人类与机器人互动；2）AI导师通过自适应干预机制与机器人互动。学习框架基于自由能原理的神经网络，并结合生成回放机制实现从单次教学序列的稳定逐序列学习。

Result: 在两种实验条件下，双向教学都促进了机器人形成一致的行为模式、实现阶段性泛化，且机器人逐渐需要更少的导师指导。双向教学相比单向教学产生了更一致的行为模式

Conclusion: 双向教学作为一种具身和社会性接地的方法，为机器人发展性运动学习提供了有效的脚手架。研究强调了社会互动在机器人技能学习中的双向适应特性，超越了传统的单向教学范式。

Abstract: Infants are well known to develop their motor skills through dense interaction with caregivers. Although such social interaction is crucial for human development, motor-skill learning in robots is often treated as a unidirectional process in which robots passively receive demonstrations from tutors. This overlooks a key property of social interaction: it is inherently bidirectional, with tutor and learner dynamically adapting to each other. In such interactions, the robot's past experiences may function as prior constraints that shape the dynamics of their co-developed trajectories. We hypothesize that bidirectional tutoring allows such constraints to guide the formation of consistent behavioral patterns that preserve behavioral coherence and support generalization, whereas unidirectional interaction lacks such constraints and leads to broader, less consistent behavioral patterns. To examine this hypothesis, we conducted two experiments with a physical humanoid robot performing an object manipulation task: one involving human-robot interaction and another employing an AI tutor interacting with the real robot through an adaptive intervention mechanism designed to examine whether similar effects would emerge under more controlled conditions. We implement the developmental learning framework using a free-energy-principle-based neural network extended with generative replay, which supports stable sequence-by-sequence learning from single tutored episodes. Across both settings, bidirectional tutoring fostered consistent behaviors and stage-wise generalization, while the robot gradually required less tutor guidance. These results suggest that bidirectional tutoring, as an embodied and socially grounded approach, provides an effective scaffold for developmental motor learning in robots.

</details>


### [316] [VOiLA: Vectorized Online Planning with Learned Diffusion Model for POMDP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19729)
*Marcus Hoerger,Rishikesh Joshi,Rahul Shome,Ian Manchester,Hanna Kurniawati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VOiLA框架，利用条件扩散模型学习POMDP的转移与观测采样器，并通过蒸馏为前馈生成器以加速在线规划。实验表明该框架在采样成本降低近三个数量级的同时，在基准测试和实体机器人上实现了优于或等同于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP虽为不确定环境下机器人规划的强有力框架，但难以获取精确的POMDP模型限制了其在现实问题中的应用。现有方法依赖手工设计的模型或大量训练数据，实用性受限。

Method: 1) 使用条件扩散模型学习任务无关的转移与观测采样器；2) 学习观测似然模型以支持基于粒子的信念更新；3) 将扩散采样器蒸馏为紧凑的前馈生成器以降低采样成本；4) 与支持GPU并行化的在线POMDP规划器VOPP集成。

Result: 蒸馏策略将采样成本降低近三个数量级；在三个基准问题上，VOiLA使用不到Recurrent Soft Actor Critic 10%的训练数据达到相等或更优性能，且对未见环境配置泛化能力更强；物理机器人实验中，仅用仿真数据训练即在10次运行中全部成功完成任务。

Conclusion: VOiLA证明了将学习生成式POMDP模型与在线规划相结合的有效性，蒸馏策略使扩散模型在机器人在线规划中的实际部署成为可能，且具有良好的样本效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty is an essential capability for autonomous robots. The Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) provides a powerful framework for such a capability. Although POMDP-based planning has advanced significantly, its application to real-world problems is often limited by the difficulty of obtaining faithful POMDP models. We present Vectorized Online planning wIth Learned diffusion model for POMDP Agents (VOiLA), a framework that learns task-agnostic POMDP models for online planning under uncertainty. VOiLA learns transition and observation samplers using conditional diffusion models and learns observation-likelihood models for particle-based belief updates. To enable efficient online planning, the diffusion samplers are distilled into compact feedforward generators and integrated with Vectorized Online POMDP Planner (VOPP), an online POMDP planner designed to leverage GPU parallelization. Experimental results indicate the distillation strategy reduces sampling cost by up to nearly three orders of magnitude, making learned generative POMDP models practical for online planning. Evaluation of VOiLA on three benchmark problems indicate that VOiLA achieves equal or better performance than Recurrent Soft Actor Critic while using less than 10% training data, and generalizes much better to unseen environment configurations. Physical robot evaluation indicates VOiLA uses the models learned using only simulated data and generates a policy that successfully accomplish the task in 10 of 10 runs.

</details>


### [317] [Temporal Self-Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19752)
*Yinsen Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出时间自我模仿学习（TSIL）框架，通过挖掘强化学习过程中产生的时间高效成功轨迹，将其转化为可重用的监督信号，从而在不依赖手工奖励塑形的情况下提升长时域机器人操作策略的学习效率和任务完成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励塑形训练的长时域机器人操作策略仍然可能通过低效交互来利用密集奖励，而罕见的高效行为可能在训练过程中被遗忘。时间效率本身是一种强大但未被充分利用的自我监督来源。

Method: TSIL框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 利用学习过程中产生的时间高效成功轨迹，构建基于配置条件的自适应时间目标，逐步优化学习过程；(2) 通过效率加权自我模仿学习，保存并重放高效行为，防止高效策略被遗忘。

Result: 在15个不同的长时域操作任务中，TSIL在以下方面表现一致提升：学习效率、任务完成效率、高效成功行为的重访频率、以及对不稳定训练条件的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 成功行为的时间结构本身提供了一种可扩展的自我监督信号，超越了手工设计的奖励塑形，为强化学习提供了新的自我监督范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation policies trained with reward shaping can still exploit dense rewards through inefficient interaction, while rare efficient behaviors may be forgotten during training. We argue that temporal efficiency itself provides a powerful and underutilized source of self-supervision for reinforcement learning. We introduce Temporal Self-Imitation Learning (TSIL), a reinforcement learning framework that mines temporally efficient successful trajectories generated during learning and converts them into reusable supervision for future policy improvement. TSIL progressively refines learning using configuration-conditioned adaptive temporal targets derived from fast successful trajectories, while preserving and replaying efficient behaviors through efficiency-weighted self-imitation learning. Across 15 distinct long-horizon manipulation tasks, TSIL consistently improves learning efficiency, task-completion efficiency, revisitation of fast successful behaviors, and robustness to unstable training conditions. More broadly, our results suggest that the temporal structure of successful behavior itself provides a scalable self-supervisory signal for reinforcement learning beyond manually engineered reward shaping alone.

</details>


### [318] [Data Standards for Humanoid Robotics: The Missing Infrastructure for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19769)
*Shaoshan Liu,Xiugong Qin,Xuan Wu,Xuan Xia,Ning Ding,Jialu Liu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于ISO/WD 26264-1标准制定工作，论证数据标准是人形机器人Physical AI的基础设施，提出人形机器人数据是具身交互数据，其价值依赖物理一致性，主要瓶颈并非数据稀缺而是数据不可累积，标准通过使具身体验可解释、可共享、可追溯、可复用来解决这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的可扩展性不仅取决于模型和硬件，还取决于物理经验能否跨机器人、任务、组织、时间进行累积。当前数据孤岛、高采集成本和评价不一致导致数据不可累积，亟需数据标准解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 基于作者在ISO/TC 299/WG 16工作组制定ISO/WD 26264-1标准（人形机器人数据集第1部分：通用要求）的实践经验，从理论层面分析人形机器人数据的本质特征，阐述数据标准如何作为Physical AI的基础设施发挥作用。

Result: 提出三个核心见解：(1)人形机器人数据是具身交互数据，需保留机器人身体、动作、任务、场景、执行轨迹与结果之间的关系；(2)数据价值取决于物理一致性——时间、坐标帧、标定、运动学、单位和同步假设需可检查；(3)主要瓶颈是数据不可累积而非数据稀缺。标准应提供水平基础设施（生命周期管理、元数据、溯源、质量、版本、可追溯性）和能力特定领域语法（操作、移动、人机交互、认知等）。

Conclusion: 随着AI从屏幕走向身体，数据标准必须从组织数字信息演进为结构化物理交互，人形机器人数据标准是Physical AI时代的基础性基础设施。

Abstract: The scalability of humanoid robots will depend not only on models and hardware, but also on whether physical experience can accumulate across robots, tasks, organizations, and time. Drawing on the authors' work in developing ISO/WD 26264-1, Humanoid robot datasets -- Part 1: General requirements, within ISO/TC 299/WG 16, this article argues that data standards are becoming foundational infrastructure for Physical AI. We develop three insights. First, humanoid robot data is embodied interaction data, not a collection of isolated digital samples; a useful dataset must preserve the relationship among robot body, action, task, scene, execution trace, and outcome. Second, its value depends on physical coherence: multimodal streams are reusable only when timing, coordinate frames, calibration, kinematics, units, and synchronization assumptions remain inspectable. Third, the main bottleneck is not only data scarcity, but non-cumulative data caused by high collection costs, data silos, and inconsistent evaluation. We argue that humanoid robot data standards address these bottlenecks by making embodied experience interpretable, shareable, traceable, and reusable. A general standard should provide horizontal infrastructure for lifecycle management, metadata, provenance, quality, versioning, and traceability, while capability-specific parts should define domain grammar for manipulation, locomotion, human-robot interaction, cognition, and future humanoid capabilities. As AI moves from screens into bodies, data standards must evolve from organizing digital information to structuring physical interaction.

</details>


### [319] [Start Right, Arrive Right: Asynchronous Execution via Initial Noise Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19774)
*Trong-Bao Ho,Quang-Tan Nguyen,Thien-Loc Ha,Gia-Binh Nguyen,Viet-Thanh Nguyen,Long Dinh,Minh N. Vu,Duy M. H. Nguyen,An Thai Le,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAINT通过反向欧拉反演选择初始噪声，实现未修改的流ODE生成连贯的下一个动作块，无需训练、梯度或策略修改，即可解决异步执行中基于流的动作分块策略的边界不一致问题，在12个模拟基准和6个真实任务上提升执行一致性和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的动作分块策略在异步执行下，生成下一动作块的延迟会导致块边界处出现不一致，现有方法通过引导生成走向已执行的动作前缀来解决，但作者提出可通过选择合适的初始噪声来实现前缀一致性。

Method: 提出PAINT，一种无需训练的方法：通过反向欧拉反演找到合适的初始噪声，并利用重绘规则构建最终的动作块，无需梯度、重新训练或策略修改。

Result: 在12个模拟基准和6个真实世界操作任务（包括单臂、双臂和人形机器人）上，PAINT提升了执行一致性和任务性能。

Conclusion: 将异步推理重新定义为噪声选择问题而非轨迹引导问题，PAINT通过训练-free的方式有效解决了基于流策略的异步执行不一致问题。

Abstract: Action chunking enables robot policies to produce temporally coherent behavior, but generating multi-step action sequences with flow-based policies incurs latency that is incompatible with real-time control. Under asynchronous execution, the robot continues executing the current chunk while the next one is generated, causing even minor delays to create inconsistencies at chunk boundaries. Existing methods address this problem by steering generation toward the already executed action prefix. We instead show that prefix consistency can be achieved by selecting an appropriate initial noise before generation begins, allowing the unmodified flow ODE to produce a coherent next chunk. This reframes asynchronous inference as a noise selection problem rather than a trajectory steering problem. We introduce \textbf{PAINT}, a training-free method that finds this noise via backward Euler inversion and constructs the final chunk through a repainting rule. In summary, \texttt{PAINT} requires no gradients, retraining, or policy modification; yet it improves execution consistency and task performance across \textit{12 simulated benchmarks} and \textit{6 real-world manipulation tasks} spanning single-arm, bimanual, and humanoid embodiments. Website: ~\href{https://paint-action-chunking.github.io}{\texttt{https://paint-action-chunking.github.io}}.

</details>


### [320] [EquiVLA: A General Framework for Rotationally Equivariant Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19784)
*Thien-Loc Ha,Quang-Tan Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Long Dinh,Minh Duc Nguyen,Gia-Binh Nguyen,Pham Tri Quang,Minh N. Vu,Duy M. H. Nguyen,An Thai Le,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiVLA是一个面向SO(2)-等变VLA模型的通用框架，通过EquiPerceptor（近似等变视觉表征）和EquiActor（精确等变动作头）实现从观测到动作的等变链，在LIBERO、CALVIN和真实机器人任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏几何归纳偏置，导致在旋转配置下泛化需要大量数据，因此需要引入等变性来提高数据效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出EquiVLA框架，包含EquiPerceptor（从冻结ViT特征生成近似SO(2)-等变视觉表征）和EquiActor（精确SO(2)-等变的流匹配扩散Transformer动作头），两者共同构成从相机观测到动作序列的近似SO(2)等变链。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到92.6%平均成功率（基线78.1%），CALVIN上平均序列长度4.03（基线3.45），真实机器人任务成功率从54%提升至72%。

Conclusion: EquiVLA作为首个端到端SO(2)-等变VLA通用框架，通过引入旋转等变归纳偏置，显著提升了机器人操纵策略的泛化能力和样本效率，在多个基准上取得了大幅性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for generalist robot manipulation, yet they lack geometric inductive biases: policies trained at specific orientations require substantially more data to generalize across rotational configurations. We present \textsc{EquiVLA}, the first general framework for end-to-end $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant VLA models, applicable to any architecture coupling a frozen vision-language backbone with a flow-matching Diffusion Transformer action head. \textsc{EquiVLA} introduces \textsc{EquiPerceptor}, which produces approximately $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant visual representations from frozen ViT features; and \textsc{EquiActor}, an exactly $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant flow-matching Diffusion Transformer action head. Together, they establish an approximate $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ equivariance chain from camera observations to predicted action sequences. Instantiated on GR00T~N1.5 and evaluated across four LIBERO suites, CALVIN ABCD$\to$D, and five real-robot tasks on Mobile ALOHA, \textsc{EquiVLA} achieves $92.6\%$ average success on LIBERO (vs. $78.1\%$ baseline), an average sequence length of $4.03$ on CALVIN (vs. $3.45$), and improves real-robot success from $54\%$ to $72\%$.

</details>


### [321] [TIDY: Thermal Infrared Image Denoising via Wavelet Domain Entropy and Directional Stripe Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19813)
*Tai Hyoung Rhee,Dong-Guw Lee,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDY，一种轻量级小波域热红外图像去噪方法，通过真实干净-噪声数据训练、小波域噪声解耦、以及波尔熵和波尔方向条纹指数两项新指标作为损失项，在室内低热对比场景下实现了快速（~34Hz）且鲁棒的去噪，显著提升了热惯性里程计和单目深度估计等下游机器人任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在低光照条件下感知鲁棒，但受随机噪声和固定模式噪声严重影响，尤其在室内低热对比环境中噪声加剧；现有去噪方法在精度与效率之间难以取得良好平衡，要么速度太慢无法用于机器人实时部署，要么对严重退化不够鲁棒，且通常使用合成噪声训练。

Method: 提出TIDY轻量级小波域去噪器：将去噪过程重构建在小波域中，显式解耦噪声与结构内容，降低空间复杂度以提升推理速度（~34Hz）；引入波尔熵（Wavelet Entropy）和波尔方向条纹指数（Wavelet Directional Stripe Index）两项新指标作为补充损失项，分别抑制随机噪声和条纹伪影；使用真实干净-噪声热红外数据训练。

Result: 在严重室内退化场景和零样本设置下，TIDY展现出更强的鲁棒性，并在热惯性里程计和单目深度估计等下游机器人任务中取得一致的性能提升，推理速度达约34Hz可满足在线部署需求。

Conclusion: TIDY通过在真实数据上的小波域去噪策略，有效解决了室内热红外成像噪声严重且现有方法精度-效率无法兼顾的问题，为机器人领域的热红外在线感知提供了一种高效鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (TIR) imaging has been a popular choice for field robotics due to its robust perception capability under low light visual degradation, but it suffers from severe stochastic and fixed-pattern noise that breaks downstream estimation. This noise is intensified indoors due to low thermal contrast and uniform temperature distributions, contributing to the relative lack of indoor TIR deployments. Existing TIR denoising methods exhibit a poor accuracy-efficiency tradeoff, either too slow for online deployment required in robotics or insufficiently robust to severe degradation, while typically being trained on synthetic noise. Addressing these problems, we propose TIDY, a lightweight wavelet-domain denoiser trained on real clean-noisy TIR data. By reformulating TIR denoising in the wavelet domain, TIDY explicitly disentangles noise from structural content, enabling targeted suppression with reduced spatial complexity, significantly improving inference speed over prior methods (~34Hz). TIDY introduces two new metrics, Wavelet Entropy and Wavelet Directional Stripe Index, as complementary loss terms to explicitly suppress stochastic noise and stripe artifacts. Across severe indoor corruption and zero-shot settings, TIDY improves robustness and yields consistent gains in downstream robotics tasks including thermal inertial odometry and monocular depth estimation. Code and dataset is available at: https://github.com/williamrheeth/TIDY

</details>


### [322] [World Engine: Towards the Era of Post-Training for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19836)
*Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Caojun Wang,Haochen Liu,Kashyap Chitta,Zhenjie Yang,Yuhang Lu,Naisheng Ye,Yihang Qiu,Yufei Wang,Luoxi Zou,Jiaxin Peng,Jin Pan,Zhaoyu Su,Andrei Bursuc,Shengbo Eben Li,Andreas Geiger,Peng Su,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World Engine框架，通过从真实驾驶日志重建高保真交互环境并系统外推至安全关键场景，对预训练驾驶模型进行后训练对齐，显著减少罕见安全关键场景下的故障，并在真实道路测试中展现出可测量的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略在常规场景表现良好，但受限于真实数据集中安全关键的"长尾事件"稀缺性，这些罕见交互定义了学习策略的实际安全边界，却难以大规模收集。

Method: 提出World Engine生成框架，从真实世界日志重建高保真交互环境，系统性地外推至现实的安全关键变体，通过基于强化学习的后训练将策略与安全约束对齐。

Result: 在nuPlan公开基准上显著减少罕见安全关键场景的故障，增益远超单独扩展预训练数据；在生产级自动驾驶系统中减少模拟碰撞，并在真实道路测试中展现出可测量的改进。

Conclusion: 在合成安全关键交互上进行后训练是实现更安全自动驾驶的可扩展且有效的途径。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must operate safely in the real world, where errors can have severe consequences. Although modern end-to-end driving policies excel in routine scenarios, their reliability is limited by the scarcity of safety-critical ``long-tail'' events in real driving datasets. These rare interactions define the practical safety boundary of the learned policy, yet they are difficult to collect at scale in the real world. Here we show that this fundamental limitation can be addressed by post-training pre-trained driving models on synthesized high-stakes interactions. We introduce World Engine, a generative framework that reconstructs high-fidelity interactive environments from real-world logs and systematically extrapolates them into realistic safety-critical variations. This paradigm enables reinforcement-based post-training to align policies with safety constraints, circumventing the physical risks inherent in real-world exploration. On a public benchmark built on nuPlan, World Engine substantially reduces failures in rare safety-critical scenarios and yields significantly larger gains than scaling pre-training data alone. Furthermore, when deployed on a production-scale autonomous driving system, the resulting policy reduces simulated collisions and demonstrates measurable improvements in on-road testing, showing that post-training on synthesized, safety-critical interactions offers a scalable and effective pathway to safer autonomous driving. The full codebase suite, including training, is released to the public.

</details>


### [323] [MMD-SLAM: Structure-Enhanced Multi-Meta Gaussian Distribution-Guided Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19874)
*Fan Zhu,Ziyu Chen,Peichen Liu,Yifan Zhao,Zhisong Xu,Hui Zhu,Hongxing Zhou,Sixun Liu,Chunmao Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MMD-SLAM提出了一种基于亚特兰大世界假设的结构增强型视觉SLAM框架，通过多元高斯表示和点线融合策略，在跟踪精度和建图质量上均达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-based SLAM系统未能充分利用场景中的结构化信息，导致渲染质量受限且地图一致性差。

Method: 1) 点线融合策略用于位姿优化，引入3D线段提升跟踪鲁棒性；2) 基于AW假设设计带主导方向的多元高斯表示，显式编码结构先验；3) 提出高斯演化策略，自适应场景几何并将结构线索融入全局优化。

Result: 在ScanNet上ATE RMSE降低48.56%，在Replica上PSNR提升5.71%，均优于MonoGS。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入结构化先验（AW假设和线特征），MMD-SLAM有效弥补了现有3DGS-SLAM在结构信息利用上的不足，显著提升了视觉SLAM的跟踪与建图性能。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has significantly boosted novel view synthesis and high-fidelity scene reconstruction, expanding the potential of 3DGS-based Visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) methods. However, most existing systems fail to fully exploit the underlying structural information, which limits rendering quality and often leads to inconsistent maps. To address these limitations, we propose MMD-SLAM, a structure-enhanced Visual SLAM framework that leverages the Atlanta World (AW) assumption to guide a Multi-Meta Gaussian representation for photorealistic mapping. First, we introduce a point-line fusion strategy for pose optimization, where 3D line segments are incorporated to improve tracking robustness and provide additional constraints for mapping. Second, we design a Multi-Meta Gaussian representation with dominant directions, explicitly encoding structural priors from the AW hypothesis. Finally, we propose a Gaussian evolution strategy that adapts to scene geometry and incorporates structural cues into global optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that these innovations enable MMD-SLAM to achieve state-of-the-art performance in both tracking accuracy and mapping quality. e.g., our method achieves a 48.56% reduction in ATE RMSE on ScanNet and a 5.71% improvement in PSNR on Replica, compared with MonoGS.

</details>


### [324] [One-to-Two Acting: A Novel Framework for Single-arm Agent Action Expansion to Dual Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19897)
*Youbin Yao,Nieqin Cao,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fuqiang Gu,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExS2D提出了一种层次化动作扩展框架，通过单臂监督数据实现双臂协作操作，无需任何双臂示范数据，在仿真实验中平均执行步骤减少54.4%，成功率与单臂基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作可通过并行执行提升效率，但收集双臂示范数据成本高昂且困难，亟需一种无需双臂示范即可实现双臂操作的方法。

Method: ExS2D框架包含三个阶段：(1) 从文本指令生成结构化子任务并显式捕获时间先后关系；(2) 通过子任务引导的动作映射将每个子任务接地到可执行动作；(3) 由多模态大语言模型驱动的协调器进行优先级感知的动作分配和同步规划，选择无碰撞的双臂执行方案。

Result: 仿真实验显示平均执行步骤减少54.4%，成功率与单臂基线相当；在四个实际机器人任务上验证了仅需少量单臂样本即可可靠执行双臂操作，且使用零双臂示范数据。

Conclusion: ExS2D有效利用单臂监督实现了高效的双臂操作，大幅降低了数据收集成本，同时保持了任务执行的成功率和效率。

Abstract: Dual-arm manipulation can improve throughput via parallel execution, but collecting bimanual demonstrations for training is costly and difficult. We present ExS2D, a hierarchical action expansion framework that enables dual-arm manipulation from single-arm supervision. ExS2D first generates structured subtasks from textual instructions while explicitly capturing temporal precedence. It then grounds each subtask into executable actions through subtask-guided action mapping in observation. Finally, precedence-aware action allocation and synchronized planning are performed by a multimodal large language model driven coordinator to select collision-free dual-arm executions. Simulation experiments demonstrate that ExS2D reduces the average execution steps by 54.4% while maintaining a comparable success rate to a single-arm baseline. Real-robot experiments on four tasks further demonstrate the reliability of ExS2D for dual-arm execution under few-shot single-arm samples, while using zero bimanual demonstrations.

</details>


### [325] [Co-policy: Responsive Human-Robot Co-Creation for Musical Performances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19914)
*Xuetao Li,Wenke Huang,Mang Ye,Zijian Liu,Jinhua Xie,Jifeng Xuan,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Co-policy框架，将语义意图理解、约束性音乐变体生成和视觉运动执行分离，实现人机音乐共创作。通过预推理语义锚点和微调Qwen-vl规划器（F-Qwen）实现语义理解，利用高斯混合视觉运动策略（GMP）实现低延迟物理执行。在真实机器人编钟实验中，Co-policy在意图对齐、执行准确性和响应频率上优于扩散策略等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接语义音乐理解与实时可物理执行的机器人音乐共创作具有挑战性，现有回放系统仅机械复现音符，缺乏基于物理动作的生成式共创能力。

Method: Co-policy框架包含三个模块：1）F-Qwen规划器将语音、现场音乐种子和视觉观测转化为结构化共创作计划；2）高斯混合视觉运动策略（GMP）作为条件混合密度策略，将目标音符和视觉上下文映射为多模态机器人动作；3）单次前向传播实现低延迟执行。

Result: 在真实机器人编钟实验中，Co-policy在意图对齐、执行准确性和响应频率上优于扩散策略和消融基线。

Conclusion: 物理基础的动作生成是实现具身人机共创作的关键要求，Co-policy通过分离语义意图与物理执行有效实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Art has long stood as a pivotal expression of human creativity. Embodied artificial intelligence offers a route for generative models to participate in that creativity through physical action rather than disembodied digital content.
