In robotic music co-creation, it is challenging to connect semantic musical understanding with real-time and physically executable performance. We present Co-policy, a framework for human-robot musical co-creation that separates semantic intent grounding, constrained musical variation, and visuomotor execution. To ground musical semantics, Co-policy uses pre-inference semantic anchors and a fine-tuned Qwen-vl planner (F-Qwen) to transform speech, live musical seeds, and visual observations into structured co-creation plans. To support low-latency execution, Co-policy introduces a Gaussian-Mixture Visuomotor Policy (GMP), implemented as a conditional mixture-density policy that maps target notes and visual context to multimodal robot actions in a single forward pass. Unlike robotic playback systems that merely reproduce user-specified notes, Co-policy generates complementary musical responses under both musical and physical constraints. Real-robot chime experiments, ablations, and expert evaluation show improved intent alignment, execution accuracy, and response frequency over diffusion-policy and ablated baselines, supporting physically grounded action generation as a key requirement for embodied human-AI co-creation.

</details>


### [326] [Deep-Unfolded Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19920)
*Hunter Kuperman,Minchan Jung,Rahul V. Ghosh,Alex Oshin,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Coordinator，一种基于深度展开的框架，能够在求解过程中动态调整ADMM-DDP分布式优化器的超参数，相比传统求解器在车队和四旋翼仿真中实现6.18-9.44倍加速，且具备良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化在多智能体机器人任务中具有可扩展性和结构透明性，但传统方法需要高度特化且问题特定的超参数调优，成本高昂且缺乏适应性。

Method: 将固定次数的ADMM-DDP迭代展开为神经网络，层间包含可学习函数，将优化器状态映射到下一轮超参数；采用无监督学习方案（避免有监督方法导致的退化解）来训练模型。

Result: 在车队和四旋翼仿真中，Deep Coordinator生成的轨迹质量相当，但速度比传统求解器快6.18-9.44倍；部署到比训练规模大8倍的系统时仍能保持性能优势。

Conclusion: Deep Coordinator是首个在求解时自适应调整非凸优化器惩罚参数的深度展开框架，证明无监督学习方案的有效性，并展现出良好的跨规模泛化能力。

Abstract: Distributed optimization is a highly scalable and structurally transparent technique to solve multi-agent robotics problems; however, such methods often suffer from the need for highly-specialized, problem-specific hyperparameter tunings. In this work, we propose Deep Coordinator, a deep-unfolding framework that learns to dynamically adjust the hyperparameters of ADMM-DDP, a popular distributed solver for robotics tasks, at solve-time in response to optimizer performance. Our architecture consists of unrolling a fixed number of ADMM-DDP iterations into a neural network with learnable functions between layers mapping the optimizer state to the next hyperparameters. To the best of our knowledge, Deep Coordinator is the first deep-unfolding framework to adapt the penalty parameters of a non-convex optimizer at solve-time; we show that the mainstream supervised approach can yield degenerate solutions when training such models, and propose an unsupervised learning scheme. On simulations with fleets of cars and quadrotors, Deep Coordinator produces trajectories of comparable quality 6.18-9.44x faster than conventional solvers. Furthermore, Deep Coordinator retains its performance benefits when deployed to systems up to 8x larger than trained on.

</details>


### [327] [SWAP: Symmetric Equivariant World-Model for Agile Robot Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19928)
*Kaixin Lan,Ze Wang,Hongyi Li,Lei Jiang,Chaojie Fu,Chengkai Su,Choi Lam Wong,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAP是一种端到端等变对称世界模型，通过将对称性直接嵌入到世界模型和演员-评论家网络中，使四足机器人能够完成2.13米跨越和1.63米平台攀爬等创纪录的跑酷动作，并在未见过的镜像地形中展现出强大的几何泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的潜空间世界模型会冗余编码左右对称交互模式，增大了学习负担并阻碍了几何规律性的捕获，限制了潜空间对下游策略的效率。

Method: 提出SWAP，一个端到端等变对称世界模型框架，将对称性直接嵌入到世界模型和演员-评论家网络中。

Result: 机器人跨越2.13米间隙并攀爬1.63米平台，创下四足机器人跑酷纪录；对未见过的镜像地形具有鲁棒的几何泛化能力；在多样户外环境中表现出卓越的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 对称等变性是一种有效的结构先验，能够推动学习型腿式运动的物理极限。

Abstract: While latent world models enable the proactive predictions required for extreme parkour, their purely data-driven nature forces them to redundantly encode left-right symmetric interactions as independent patterns. This inflates the learning burden and hinders the capture of geometric regularities, restricting the latent space's efficiency for downstream policies. To address this, we propose SWAP, an end-to-end equivariant symmetric world model. This framework embeds symmetry directly into both the world model and the actor-critic networks. In real-world tests, the robot leaps across a 2.13 m gap and climbs a 1.63 m platform, breaking records for quadruped parkour. Furthermore, the framework exhibits robust geometric generalization to unseen mirrored terrains and exceptional zero-shot transferability across diverse outdoor environments. These results demonstrate that symmetry equivariance is an effective structural prior for pushing the physical boundaries of learned legged locomotion.

</details>


### [328] [Motor Angular Speed Preintegration for Multirotor UAV State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19929)
*Matěj Petrlík,Filip Novák,Robert Pěnička,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于电机转速预积分替代IMU惯性测量的方法，实现更精确的UAV状态估计，MAS-LO算法相比LIO-SAM在位置精度提升28%，速度精度提升65%，测量延迟降低14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV进行敏捷飞行和近障碍飞行时对精确状态估计需求极高；现有方法融合低频位姿测量与高频IMU测量，但螺旋桨振动会降低IMU精度，影响估计状态质量。

Method: 提出基于电机转速的加速度预积分方法替代IMU进行状态传播；设计预积分电机转速因子，可直接用于因子图优化框架；结合LiDAR测量，提出MAS-LO算法。

Result: 相比于SOTA惯性算法LIO-SAM，位置估计精度提升28%，速度估计精度提升65%，测量延迟降低14%，对错误参数值具有高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 电机转速预积分能够有效替代IMU进行状态估计，规避螺旋桨振动干扰，显著提升UAV状态估计精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: A precise state estimate is crucial for a tight feedback control that enables agile and near-obstacle flights of UAVs. The state-of-the-art methods fuse slow pose measurements with high-frequency inertial measurements to obtain a precise state estimate. However, the inertial measurements from the IMU onboard the UAV are degraded by vibrations from spinning propellers and the precision of the estimated state suffers. We propose a novel approach based on the preintegration of accelerations obtained from motor speeds. We show that the accelerations obtained in this manner can be used for state propagation on their own to achieve better precision without including the IMU. Further, we propose a factor composed of the preintegrated motor speeds that can be directly employed in factor graph optimization frameworks. We combine our factor with LiDAR measurements into the proposed Motor Angular Speed LiDAR Odometry (MAS-LO) algorithm for precise state estimation, which we open-source. Lastly, we evaluate the estimation precision against a state-of-the-art inertial algorithm LIO-SAM to show 28% improvement in position and 65% in velocity estimation accuracy, 14% lower measurement lag, and high robustness to wrong parameter values.

</details>


### [329] [Evaluation of Augmented Reality-based Intuitive Interface for Robot-Assisted Transesophageal Echocardiography: A User Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19971)
*Xiu Zhang*,Matteo Di Mauro*,Sofia Breschi,Angela Peloso,Emiliano Votta,Arianna Menciassi,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出并评估了一种基于增强现实（AR）的直观界面用于机器人辅助经食管超声心动图（TEE），通过比较三种用户界面（2D关节级、3D关节级和3D尖端级），发现3D尖端级界面在空间精度、操作效率和用户负荷方面表现最优，位置误差中位数从13mm降至3mm，方向误差减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动TEE操作对操作者技能要求高、体力消耗大，且在联合荧光镜检查时存在辐射暴露风险。虽然机器人辅助TEE系统已引入以改善操作并减轻疲劳，但直观有效的用户界面设计仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 在机器人TEE平台上集成电磁追踪和虚拟模拟器，设计了三种用户界面：2D关节级（2D-JI）、3D关节级（3D-JI）和3D尖端级（3D-TI）。36名参与者执行标准化导航任务以重现目标超声心动图视图，通过位置和方向误差、完成时间及NASA-TLX工作量评分评估性能。

Result: 3D可视化显著提高了空间精度，位置误差中位数从13mm降至3mm，方向误差减半。尖端级交互相比关节级控制将方向误差进一步降低50%，并减少了用户间变异性。3D-TI配置（沉浸式可视化+直接尖端控制）证明是最有效且最符合人体工学的界面。

Conclusion: 支持将AR增强的可视化和直观控制范式集成到下一代机器人TEE系统中，以提升操作者表现和手术安全性。

Abstract: TransEsophageal Echocardiography (TEE) is essential for diagnosing and guiding Structural Heart Disease (SHD) interventions. However, manual TEE manipulation demands significant operator expertise, is physically demanding, and exposes clinicians to radiation when performed alongside fluoroscopy. Robotic-assisted TEE systems have been introduced to improve probe handling and reduce operator fatigue, yet the design of intuitive and effective user interfaces remains an open challenge. This study presents and evaluates a model-enhanced, Augmented Reality (AR)-based intuitive interface for robot-assisted TEE, designed to improve spatial awareness and control intuitiveness. A robotic TEE platform integrated with electromagnetic tracking and a virtual simulator was used to compare three user interfaces differing in visualization and interaction modalities: 2D jointlevel (2D-JI), 3D joint-level (3D-JI), and 3D tip-level (3D-TI). Thirty six participants performed standardized navigation tasks to reproduce target echocardiographic views, with performance assessed via position and orientation errors, completion time, and NASA-TLX workload scores. Results show that 3D visualization significantly improved spatial accuracy, reducing median position error from 13 mm to 3 mm and halving the orientation error compared with the 2D interface. Tip-level interaction yielded a further 50% reduction in orientation error and reduced interuser variability relative to joint-level control. Overall, the 3D-TI configuration, combining immersive visualization with direct tip-level control, proved the most effective and ergonomic interface, supporting the integration of AR-based visualization and intuitive control paradigms into next-generation robotic TEE systems to enhance operator performance and procedural safety.

</details>


### [330] [Tri-Info: Generalizable, Interpretable Failure Prediction for VLA Models via Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19998)
*Jinghan Yang,Yunchao Zhang,Wang Yuan,Haolun Wan,Jiaming Zhang,Zhengyang Hu,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tri-Info方法，通过三个信息论信号（动作多样性、时间一致性、状态耦合性）检测VLA模型在执行任务时的失败情况。该方法在六种VLA模型和三种基准环境中表现优异，无需重新训练即可跨架构、跨环境、跨仿真-真实场景迁移，在真实任务中达到83%准确率，远超基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在物理交互中可能造成不可逆伤害，现有黑箱模型缺乏可泛化且可解释的失败检测机制。

Method: 将VLA控制形式化为闭环信息管道，推导出三个信息论信号（Tri-Info）：动作多样性、时间一致性、状态-动作耦合性，用于检测失败。

Result: 在六种VLA模型和三种基准环境中，Tri-Info与最强基线性能相当；跨架构、环境及仿真-真实迁移无需重新训练，在真实任务中达83%准确率，而基线模型降至随机水平。

Conclusion: Tri-Info是一种简单而强大的方法，不仅能强跨域泛化地检测失败，还能提供可解释的失败模式诊断。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly deployed across diverse tasks, yet they remain black boxes whose physical interactions can cause irreversible harm, making generalizable and interpretable failure detection essential. We observe that successful and failed rollouts carry systematically different information-theoretic signatures. Building on this, we formalize VLA control as a closed-loop information pipeline and derive the Triple Information-theoretic (Tri-Info) signals that capture whether actions remain diverse, temporally consistent, and coupled to state transitions. Across six VLA models and three benchmark environments, Tri-Info matches the strongest baselines in-domain. Moreover, Tri-Info transfers across architectures, environments, and the sim-to-real gap without retraining, reaching 83\% accuracy on real-world tasks where prior detectors collapse to chance. This establishes Tri-Info as a simple yet powerful method that not only detects failures with strong cross-domain generalization, but also delivers interpretable diagnostics of the underlying failure modes.

</details>


### [331] [A Neuromorphic Reinforcement Learning Framework for Efficient Pathfinding in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20031)
*Junzhe Xu,Zecui Zeng,Lusong Li,Yuetong Fang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SDQN-RMFS框架，将强化学习训练的ANN策略通过知识蒸馏转换为SNN，部署到神经形态芯片上，在RMFS路径规划中实现高达11281倍能耗节省和近两倍延迟降低，同时保持决策质量不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMFS路径规划面临动态环境变化、受限工作空间和严格实时约束，传统搜索和规则方法计算复杂度高、决策延迟大；RL虽有效，但在资源受限硬件上高能效部署仍具挑战。

Method: 采用碰撞允许策略训练ANN策略以稠密化信息轨迹，再通过硬标签知识蒸馏将ANN转换为SNN，解决输出分布不匹配问题，最终部署到神经形态芯片上，实现事件驱动、稀疏触发的超低功耗推理。

Result: 相比高性能GPU基线，实现高达11281倍能耗节省，延迟降低近两倍，同时决策质量与原始训练策略持平。

Conclusion: 物理神经形态推理可作为大规模RMFS运营中实用且节能的路径，为资源受限硬件上的RL策略部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic environmental changes, confined workspaces, and stringent real-time constraints make pathfinding in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems (RMFS) a challenging problem for conventional search- and rule-based methods, which typically suffer from high computational complexity and long decision latency. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, deploying learned policies with extreme energy efficiency on resource-constrained hardware remains an open challenge. We present SDQN-RMFS, an end-to-end framework that achieves high-fidelity deployment of an RL-trained policy from a full-precision artificial neural network (ANN) through to a neuromorphic chip. By computing only when triggered by sparse events, this framework unlocks ultra-low-power RMFS pathfinding. Our full-stack pipeline operates as follows: an ANN policy is first efficiently trained via a collision-allowing strategy to densify informative trajectories, and then converted into a spiking neural network (SNN) via a hard-label knowledge distillation approach. This effectively addresses the output distribution mismatch, preserving policy capability across the ANN-to-SNN pipeline while substantially reducing inference latency. Hardware experiments demonstrate up to 11,281$\times$ energy savings and a nearly two-fold reduction in latency compared to a high-performance GPU baseline, while maintaining decision quality on par with the original trained policy. These results establish physical neuromorphic inference as a practical and energy-sustainable pathway for large-scale RMFS operations.

</details>


### [332] [MirrorDuo: Reflection-Consistent Visuomotor Learning from Mirrored Demonstration Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20048)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDuo通过镜像增强技术，将每一条原始演示数据自动生成对应的镜像版本，实现“采集一条，免费获得一条”，有效提升行为克隆在操作空间变化下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的行为克隆受限于收集多样化演示数据的高成本，尤其是在需要泛化到不同工作空间变化场景时。为解决这一数据效率瓶颈，作者希望探索一种低成本、高效的演示数据利用方式。

Method: 提出MirrorDuo框架，基于反射对称性原理，对图像、本体感知和末端执行器完整6-DoF动作三元组进行镜像变换，生成每条原始演示的镜像对应版本。该方法可作为数据增强策略集成到现有行为克隆或扩散策略学习流程中，也可作为反射等变策略网络的结构先验。

Result: 在相同数据预算下，当演示数据均匀分布在工作空间两侧时，MirrorDuo显著提升了任务性能；当演示数据仅局限在一侧时，MirrorDuo仅需0到5条目标配置演示即可实现高效的技能迁移。

Conclusion: MirrorDuo通过反射对称性有效利用原始与镜像域之间的重叠信息，在不增加实际数据采集成本的前提下大幅提升行为克隆的泛化能力和数据效率。

Abstract: Image-based behaviour cloning leverages demonstrations captured from ubiquitous RGB cameras. However, it remains constrained by the cost of collecting diverse demos, especially for generalizing across workspace variations. We propose MirrorDuo, a reflection-based formulation that operates on image, proprioception, and full 6-DoF end-effector action tuples, generating a mirrored counterpart for each original demonstration, effectively achieving "collect one, get one for free". It can be applied as a data augmentation strategy for existing learning pipelines, such as standard behaviour cloning or diffusion policy, or as a structural prior for reflection-equivariant policy networks. By leveraging the overlap between the original and mirrored domains, MirrorDuo achieves significantly improved performance under the same data budget when demonstrations are evenly distributed across both sides of the workspace. When demonstrations are confined to one side, MirrorDuo enables efficient skill transfer to the mirrored workspace with as few as zero or five demos in the target arrangement.

</details>


### [333] [VFILC: Accurate Frequency Extrapolations in Imitation Learning via Sampling Frequency ILC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20056)
*Nozomu Masuya,Toshiaki Tsuji,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合可变频率模仿学习(VFIL)和迭代学习控制(ILC)的方法(VFILC)，通过前馈和反馈两部分实现外推速度的精确模仿，显著降低频率误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于神经网络的模仿学习方法在变速度运动中，要么仅限于插值速度，要么在外推超出训练速度范围时产生不可预测的运动。VFIL虽能通过将模型采样频率与运动频率关联实现速度外推，但其开环配置导致频率误差，尤其是在外推高频设置下。

Method: 提出VFILC方法，结合VFIL（前馈部分）和ILC（反馈部分），前者负责速度外推，后者通过迭代学习调整频率误差。

Result: 在所有三个任务中成功且准确地外推了运动速度并降低了频率误差；在双倍平均速度外推时，反馈部分在擦拭任务中降低频率误差81%，在摇动任务中降低50%；在受复杂摩擦影响的接触式混合任务中，即使在内插频率下，精度也比VFIL提高了27%。

Conclusion: 所提出的VFILC方法能够有效实现外推速度的精确模仿学习，通过反馈机制显著改善频率误差问题。

Abstract: Conventional neural network (NN)-based imitation learning methods for variable-speed motion either restricted their scope to interpolated speeds, or generated unpredictable motions when extrapolating beyond trained velocity ranges. Variable-frequency imitation learning (VFIL) enabled extrapolations of speeds by linking the NN model's sampling frequency to the motion frequency, whereas its open-loop configuration caused frequency errors, especially in the extrapolated high-frequency settings. This study proposes variable-frequency imitation learning with iterative learning control (VFILC) based on a combination of VFIL and iterative learning control (ILC) with both feedforward and feedback parts, the former taking advantage of VFIL and the latter adjusting the frequency errors. The experimental results showed that the proposed method successfully and accurately extrapolated motion speeds and reduced frequency errors in all three tasks, and that the feedback especially reduced the frequency errors by a remarkable 81% in the wiping task and 50% in the shaking task, both compared to simple feedforward VFIL, when extrapolating at double the average speed in the training data. The proposed method also improved accuracy by 27% compared with VFIL even at an interpolated frequency for a contact-rich mixing task affected by complex friction traits.

</details>


### [334] [Pose6DAug: Physically Plausible Multi-view Object Swapping for Robot Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20118)
*Jonghoon Lee,Seong Hyeon Park,Byungwoo Jeon,Minha Lee,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Pose6DAug，一种基于失败驱动的数据增强框架，通过将成功轨迹中的物体替换为失败案例中的物体并保持6D位姿一致性，生成多视图物理合理的演示数据，无需额外数据收集即可提升VLA策略在新物体上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在训练分布外的陌生物体上泛化失败，传统收集多视图遥操作数据的方案成本高、扩展性差。

Method: 利用成功轨迹中已有的物理有效动作和标定多视图观察，仅替换操作物体并保持轨迹不变；通过显式网格和时序一致6D位姿轨迹在3D空间操作，确保多视图几何一致性和物理合理性，生成新演示数据进行微调。

Result: 在新物体上相比SOTA基线提升16.5%的成功率，同时保持分布内性能不下降。

Conclusion: 多视图物理一致的数据增强是实现VLA策略可扩展泛化的实用路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for general-purpose manipulation, yet they often fail on novel, out-of-distribution objects whose appearance or geometry deviates from the training distribution. The standard remedy is to collect multi-view teleoperation data for every failure case, but this scales poorly in both cost and time. We introduce Pose6DAug, a failure-driven data augmentation framework that turns a policy's own successful episodes into targeted demonstrations for its failure modes, without any new data collection. Our key insight is that each successful episode already encodes a physically valid action trajectory together with calibrated multi-view observations. By swapping only the manipulated object while preserving this trajectory, we obtain new and physically grounded demonstrations. However, naive 2D video editing breaks multi-view consistency and physical plausibility, particularly under heavy occlusion and egocentric viewpoints. Our method instead operates directly in 3D, anchoring the target object with an explicit mesh driven by a temporally coherent 6D pose trajectory, ensuring geometrically consistent renderings across all camera views. Fine-tuning a VLA on data augmented by our method improves success rates by 16.5% relative to the state-of-the-art baseline on novel objects, while preserving in-distribution performance. These results show that multi-view and physically consistent augmentation is a practical path to scalable VLA generalization.

</details>


### [335] [Dual-Agent Framework for Cross-Model Verified Translation of Natural-Language Protocols into Robotic Laboratory Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20120)
*Hyeonna Choi,Jung Yup Kim,Hyuneui Lim,Seunggyu Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体的协议翻译框架，将自然语言的微孔板实验协议自动转换为机器人平台的设备级控制命令，并通过异构LLM验证与自校正循环确保翻译准确性，最终在Bradford蛋白定量实验中验证了端到端自主执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验协议使用自然语言编写，而自动化系统依赖预定义控制命令，两者之间的语义鸿沟限制了实验的自主执行。微孔板自动实验尤其复杂，需要同时控制孔映射、样本-试剂组合、重复放置和平行分配。

Method: 提出智能体协议翻译框架：Parser Agent将自然语言协议形式化为结构化表示；基于规则的映射引擎确定性整合机器人平台操作约束生成设备级命令；异构LLM Validation Agent验证完整性、参数准确性和执行顺序，检测错误时触发带结构化反馈的自校正循环。在ELISA协议上进行了7种Parser和3种Validator的交叉验证评估。

Result: 通过交叉模型验证评估模型规模和验证器类型对翻译精度和通过率的影响；通过规则映射与LLM端到端直接映射的对比验证了精度-延迟权衡；在机器人平台上成功演示了Bradford蛋白定量实验，验证了从自然语言协议到真实实验的端到端自主执行。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种灵活的方法来缩小自然语言协议与微孔板自驱动实验室之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Biological experiment protocols are written in natural language, whereas automation systems rely on predefined control commands, creating a semantic gap that limits autonomous execution. Microplate-based automatic experiments are particularly challenging due to the need to simultaneously control well mapping, sample-reagent combinations, replicate placement, and parallel dispensing. This study proposes an agent-based protocol translation framework that converts natural-language microplate-based protocols into executable control commands for a robotic laboratory platform. A Parser Agent formalizes the natural-language protocol into a structured representation, and a rule-based mapping engine deterministically incorporates the operational constraints of the robotic laboratory platform to generate device-level control commands. A heterogeneous LLM Validation Agent verifies completeness, parameter accuracy, and execution order, and triggers a self-correction loop with structured feedback when errors are detected. A sweep involving 7 Parsers and 3 Validators on randomly selected ELISA protocols evaluates how model scale and Validator type affect translation accuracy and pass rates under cross-model verification. The accuracy-latency trade-off is further verified by comparing the rule-based mapping of the proposed framework with LLM end-to-end direct mapping. Finally, Bradford assay-based protein quantification using a microplate was demonstrated on a robotic laboratory platform, validating end-to-end autonomous execution from natural-language protocols to real-world experiments. The proposed framework provides a flexible approach to narrowing the semantic gap between natural-language protocols and microplate-based self-driving laboratories.

</details>


### [336] [Frequency-Aware Flow Matching for Continuous and Consistent Robotic Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20135)
*Jianing Guo,Fangzheng Chen,Zihao Mao,Wong Lik Hang Kenny,Zhenhong Wu,Yu Li,Yishuai Cai,Yuanpei Chen,Yikun Ban,Kai Chen,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Huijie Zhao,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频率感知流匹配（FAFM），通过离散余弦变换将动作序列转换到频域进行流匹配，并添加Sobolev型平滑约束，实现连续、时间一致性动作生成，在多个基准和真实机器人上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配和扩散策略依赖离散化动作块，对异构控制频率的演示数据脆弱，且易产生时间不一致动作，影响控制稳定性。

Method: 使用离散余弦变换（DCT）将离散动作序列转换到频域，在频域系数上进行流匹配，再通过余弦基展开重建连续动作；同时引入一阶时间导数的正则化（Sobolev型约束）抑制高频误差和突变。

Result: 在合成玩具基准、避障、LapGym、LIBERO以及真实Franka机器人上，FAFM提升了成功率、多模态表达能力、动作平滑性、收敛速度、对机械偏差和混合频率输入的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FAFM简单且无需额外网络参数，可独立应用于流匹配策略及视觉-语言动作模型，实现连续时间一致性动作生成。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as a standard paradigm for robotic manipulation owing to its strong expressive power for modelling complex, multimodal action distributions, alongside similar approaches like diffusion policy. However, existing methods rely on discretized action chunks, making them brittle to demonstrations collected at heterogeneous control frequencies and prone to temporally inconsistent actions that degrade control stability. In this paper, we propose Frequency-Aware Flow Matching (FAFM), which outputs continuous, temporally consistent actions. To handle heterogeneous frequency input, we transform discrete action sequences into the frequency domain with the discrete cosine transform (DCT), perform flow matching over the resulting coefficients, and reconstruct continuous actions via cosine basis expansion. To generate temporally consistent actions, we regularize the first-order temporal derivative to promote smooth actions. This corresponds to a Sobolev-type constraint that suppresses high-frequency errors and discourages abrupt action changes. Our FAFM is simple, introduces no additional network parameters and applies to standalone flow-matching policies and vision-language action models. Across synthetic toy benchmark, obstacle avoidance, LapGym, and LIBERO, FAFM improves success rates, multimodal expressivity, motion smoothness, convergence speed, robustness to mechanical bias and mixed-frequency input. These gains are consistent when deployed on a real-world Franka robot. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FAFM.

</details>


### [337] [Robust Assembly State Reasoning from Action Recognition for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20150)
*James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在人体动作识别（HAR）输入下，用于跟踪装配状态的多种方法（逻辑法、隐马尔可夫模型HMM、神经网络NN），发现最优方法因任务而异：NN和HMM在有限变异性任务中表现良好，而逻辑法在更复杂场景中更稳健，且建模预期动作持续时间对重复动作任务至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR在人机协作（HRC）中常用于理解执行的动作和协作任务状态，但从HAR准确跟踪装配状态尚未被充分研究，且在实际场景中并非易事，因此需要系统比较不同跟踪方法。

Method: 使用两个多样化数据集和五种状态跟踪方法（包括逻辑法、HMM和神经网络方法），在模拟输入（不同噪声级别）和来自HAR模型的真实输入下进行测试，系统比较各方法的性能。

Result: NN和HMM方法在变异性有限的任务中表现良好，但在其他场景下逻辑法更为稳健；不同方法在不同任务和错误情境下的失效模式不同；建模预期动作持续时间对于缺乏额外传感信息的重复动作任务很关键。

Conclusion: 不存在适用于所有任务的统一最优跟踪方法，需根据任务特性选择合适的跟踪策略；逻辑法在复杂场景中更具鲁棒性，而时序建模对重复动作任务至关重要。

Abstract: Human Action Recognition (HAR) is frequently investigated in Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) research to understand what actions have been performed and hence the state of a collaborative task. Accurately tracking an assembly state from HAR is however not fully investigated, and in realistic scenarios is not a trivial task. This research systematically investigates and compares methods for tracking assembly state using action recognition inputs. Investigations using two diverse datasets and five state tracking approaches, including logic-based, Hidden Markov Model (HMM), and neural network (NN) methods, show that optimal approaches are not uniform across different tasks and that different methods fail under different circumstances. Testing is performed using both simulated inputs with varying noise levels and realistic inputs from a HAR model. Results show NN and HMM methods can perform well in tasks with limited variability, but for other scenarios logic-based approaches can be more robust. Methods which model expected action duration are also important for tasks with repeated actions where no additional sensing is provided.

</details>


### [338] [Belt-Finger: An Affordable Soft Belt-Driven Gripper for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20193)
*Boya Zhang,Andreas Zell,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双软带手指模块，为平行夹爪增加三个自由度（平移、俯仰、滚动），在保持平行夹爪简单可靠优点的同时显著提升灵巧操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行夹爪虽然简单可靠且成本低，但手内移动性有限，导致需要大幅度的机械臂运动，且在受限工作空间内难以进行灵巧操作。

Method: 设计了双软带式手指模块作为平行夹爪的升级方案，保留标准开合功能的同时增加三个手内自由度（平移、俯仰、滚动）。该机构简单、制造成本低、易于集成。将其集成到两个控制管线中：1）基于模型预测控制（MPC）的已知物体手内操作；2）轻量级遥操作接口，实现机械臂和夹爪共10自由度的同步控制。

Result: 在遥操作、MPC和训练策略下的一系列具有挑战性的操作任务中，与传统平行夹爪相比，所提出的夹爪持续提升了灵巧性和任务可行性。

Conclusion: 该设计在保持传统平行夹爪可靠性和精确控制的同时，大幅拓宽了操作能力范围，验证了通过简单机构升级即可有效提升机器人灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: Parallel-jaw grippers are the default manipulator choice in robotics because they are simple, robust, and inexpensive. Their limited in-hand mobility, however, often forces large arm motions and restricts dexterous manipulation in confined workspaces. We present a parallel-gripper upgrade: a double-soft-belt-based finger module that preserves standard opening/closing while adding three in-hand degrees of freedom (DoF): translation, pitch, and roll. The mechanism is deliberately kept simple and engineered for inexpensive manufacturing and straightforward integration, preserving the reliability and precise control of traditional parallel grippers while greatly broadening the range of manipulation capabilities. To demonstrate the utility of the added DoFs, we integrate the gripper in two control pipelines. First, we adapt a model predictive controller for in-hand manipulation of known objects. Second, we introduce a lightweight teleoperation interface that enables simultaneous control of the robot arm and gripper (10 DoFs total) with minimal hardware. Across a suite of challenging manipulation tasks executed via teleoperation, MPC, and trained policies, the proposed gripper consistently improves dexterity and task feasibility compared to a conventional parallel gripper

</details>


### [339] [Stable Transformer-Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control: A Contraction Analysis Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20197)
*Antonio Marino,Valerio Modugno,Marco Cognetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC架构，通过证明Transformer网络满足全局增量输入-状态稳定性(δISS)，并利用黎曼收缩理论分析物理系统与预测神经网络之间的互联动力学，最终将理论界作为训练正则化项，为序列预测模型在MPC中的闭环稳定性提供了形式化鲁棒性保证，并在非线性3D无人机模型上验证了目标到达与避障任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actor-Critic MPC能有效处理复杂非凸控制问题，但序列学习模型在该框架中的闭环稳定性保证仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 1) 证明Transformer网络可满足全局增量输入-状态稳定性(δISS)；2) 利用黎曼收缩理论分析物理系统与预测神经网络之间的互联动态；3) 将上述理论界作为训练正则化项，生成可认证鲁棒的策略。

Result: 在非线性3D无人机模型上进行目标到达与避障机动验证，证明了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC架构通过形式化鲁棒性保证解决了序列学习模型在MPC中的闭环稳定性挑战。

Abstract: Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control (MPC) effectively addresses complex, non-convex control problems, but guaranteeing the closed-loop stability of sequence-based learning models within these pipelines remains challenging. This paper introduces a novel Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC architecture with formal robustness guarantees. First, we prove that Transformer networks can satisfy global incremental Input-to-State Stability ($δ$ISS). We then leverage Riemannian contraction theory to analyze the interconnected dynamics between the physical plant and the predictive neural network. Finally, we integrate these theoretical bounds as a training regularizer to yield a certifiably robust policy. The framework is validated on a nonlinear 3D drone model executing target-reaching and obstacle-avoidance maneuvers.

</details>


### [340] [FlowMaps: Modeling Long-Term Multimodal Object Dynamics with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20209)
*Francesco Argenziano,Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Sacha Morin,Charlie Gauthier,Daniele Nardi,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowMaps，一种潜在流匹配模型，用于估计连续3D空间中动态物体未来位置的多模态分布。该模型通过学习物体间的隐式依赖关系及其时间演变，预测物体在人类交互后可能的位置变化，并能在未见过的环境中泛化。在600多个episodes的动态物体导航任务中，FlowMaps优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中物体位置随时间动态变化，但变化并非随机——人类习惯和日常活动产生时空一致的模式。机器人需要学习并利用这些模式，以可靠地关联当前观察与之前见过的物体，从而提升导航等下游任务的表现。

Method: FlowMaps采用潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）模型，在连续3D空间中估计动态物体未来位置的多模态分布。模型学习物体间的隐式依赖关系及其时间演变规律，以过去的人类交互为条件预测物体位置的可能变化，并支持跨未见环境的泛化。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境的600多个episodes的动态物体导航任务中，FlowMaps优于当前最先进方法，验证了通过连续、多模态的时空分布建模物体动态能够提升机器人在变化家庭环境中的搜索与导航能力。

Conclusion: 建模物体动态的连续多模态时空分布可有效提升机器人在动态家庭环境中的搜索与导航性能，FlowMaps为机器人理解空间-时间联合演化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Joint spatial and temporal understanding of 3D scenes is a crucial requirement for robots deployed in everyday household environments. Such agents must not only comprehend and navigate spatial layouts, but also reason about how these spaces evolve over time. In particular, humans interact with objects daily, causing them to change position throughout the environment and making it difficult for robots to reliably associate current observations with previously seen objects. However, these interactions are not random: human habits and routines induce spatio-temporally consistent patterns in object locations, which robotic agents can potentially learn and then exploit for downstream tasks such as navigation. To this end, we introduce FlowMaps, a latent flow matching model for estimating multimodal distributions over the future locations of dynamic objects in a continuous 3D space. By learning the implicit dependencies among objects and their temporal evolution, FlowMaps predicts likely changes in object locations conditioned on past human interactions, while supporting generalization across previously unseen environments that share similar object routines. To demonstrate the utility of this method, we deploy FlowMaps in a downstream dynamic Object Navigation task in both simulated and real-world environments. Across more than 600 episodes, FlowMaps outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, showing that modeling object dynamics through continuous, multimodal spatio-temporal distributions improves robotic search and navigation in changing household environments. Code and additional material is available at https://fra-tsuna.github.io/flowmaps/.

</details>


### [341] [Mobile Target Search with Imperfect Perception: A Partially Observable Stochastic Game Theoretical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20232)
*Hanzheng Zhang,Shu Liang,Shuyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究不完美感知下的移动目标搜索问题，采用部分可观察随机博弈（POSG）框架，提出可检测性概念和基于KL散度的服务器辅助分布式算法，并通过仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多假设完美感知或仅考虑单一搜索者，而实际场景中传感器限制、恶意干扰和通信噪声导致感知不完美，且目标具有智能逃逸行为，亟需一种能同时处理感知不确定性和目标对抗性的搜索理论框架。

Method: 采用POSG框架建模搜索者与目标之间的对抗交互；提出可检测性概念判断搜索策略能否保证最终检测，基于随机递归分析给出充分判据；开发服务器辅助分布式算法，利用聚合势博弈结构协调搜索者，使用KL散度约简进行目标预测。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提算法的有效性，并支持了可检测性分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为不完美感知下的移动目标搜索问题提供了理论保证和实用算法，POSG框架和可检测性分析可推广至更复杂的多智能体搜索场景。

Abstract: This paper investigates mobile target search under imperfect perceptions caused by sensor limitations, malicious jamming, or communication noise. Searchers and targets operate in a grid-shaped area with bounded mobility, leading to a dynamic interplay between search and evasion. To capture this adversarial interaction under imperfect perceptions, we adopt the partially observable stochastic game (POSG) approach, which generalizes partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) by incorporating target intelligence. To handle false alarms and missed detections caused by perceptual uncertainties, we propose a novel detectability concept to determine whether a search strategy guarantees eventual detection, and provide sufficient detectability criteria based on stochastic recurrence analysis. We further develop a server-assisted distributed algorithm that utilizes the aggregative potential game structure for searchers and a KL-divergence-based reduction for target prediction. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm and support the detectability analysis.

</details>


### [342] [Finetuning Vision-Language-Action Models Requires Fewer Layers Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20246)
*Gia-Binh Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Thien-Loc Ha,Khoa Vo,Philip Lund Møller,Quang T. Nguyen,Long Dinh,Tuan Dam,Vu Duong,Tung M. Luu,Trung Le,Tran Nguyen Le,Minh Vu,An Thai Le,Ngan Le,Daniel Sonntag,James Zou,Jan Peters,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示VLA模型（如π₀、GR00T-N1.5）存在严重的层级表征冗余，提出一种无需训练的压缩方法，通过单次前向传递利用Centered Kernel Alignment识别冗余层并移除孪生层，可将模型深度压缩50%，在保持或超越原始性能的同时，实现40-50%训练加速和30%推理加速，在3个模拟基准和10个真实世界任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型参数量巨大（数十亿级别），在下游微调和实时推理中计算负担过重，亟需一种高效的结构压缩方法来降低计算开销。

Method: 提出一种完全无需训练的压缩流水线：仅通过单次前向传播，利用Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）识别冗余层特征，然后永久移除孪生层（twin layers），实现模型深度最高50%的压缩，同时压缩VLM骨干和连续控制策略头。

Result: 在3个模拟基准（LIBERO、RoboCasa、SimplerEnv）和10个真实世界操作任务（4种机器人平台）上验证，压缩后模型在微调中训练时间减少40-50%，实时推理速度提升30%，性能持平或超越原始全尺寸模型。

Conclusion: 先进的VLA模型所需的层数远少于此前假设，该工作为可扩展的机器人学习提供了一种高计算效率的新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on massive video-robot datasets have revolutionized robotic manipulation, yet their multi-billion parameter architectures impose prohibitive computational burdens during downstream fine-tuning and real-time inference. In this work, we reveal a highly non-trivial architectural characteristic of these continuous control foundation policies (e.g., pi_0, GR00T-N1.5): despite being trained on diverse physical trajectories, they exhibit severe layer-wise representational redundancy. To exploit this, we introduce a structural compression pipeline that is entirely training-free, bypassing the need of existing methods to load full-scale models to learn optimized token reductions or dynamic layer selectors. Instead, using only a single forward pass via Centered Kernel Alignment to identify redundant layer features, we remove twin layers to permanently compress the model depth by up to 50% across both the VLM backbone and the continuous control policy head. Downstream fine-tuning of this streamlined architecture yields a dual acceleration benefit: a 40-50% reduction in training time and up to 30% faster real-time inference, while matching or exceeding full-scale base model performance. We comprehensively validate our method across three simulation benchmarks (LIBERO, RoboCasa, SimplerEnv) and 10 diverse real-world manipulation tasks across 4 unique robotic embodiments. These results prove that advanced VLAs require significantly fewer layers than previously assumed, offering a highly compute-efficient paradigm for scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [343] [Efficiently Linking Real Scenes with Synthetic Data Generation for AI-based Cognitive Robotics and Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20272)
*Paul Koch,Vivek Chavan,André Sers,Adem Karakurt,Paul Hofmann,Mohamad Zaher Ziadeh,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文讨论了AI视觉模型在认知机器人领域的应用现状、挑战（包括领域差距、精度限制和可扩展性问题），并介绍了作者正在进行的弥合仿真与现实世界之间领域差距的研究工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视觉模型在认知机器人（包括工业和家庭应用）中具有巨大潜力，但现有方法在训练数据、AI架构、精度和可扩展性方面面临挑战，需要更强效的方法来克服这些限制。

Method: 本文通过对相关最新技术进行综述性讨论，识别当前限制和趋势，并介绍作者正在进行的工作——通过在训练数据生成中连接仿真与现实世界来弥合领域差距。

Result: 尚未给出具体实验结果，本文为进展中的工作报告，重点在于讨论当前挑战和提出弥合仿真-现实领域差距的研究方向。

Conclusion: AI视觉模型在认知机器人中的应用需要更高效的数据生成和架构方法，通过连接仿真与现实世界的训练数据生成是弥合领域差距、提升可扩展性和精度的关键途径。

Abstract: AI vision models are a driving factor for the potential use case scenarios of cognitive robotics within in the industry and household applications. A large array of methods from semantic environment analysis towards 6D and grasping pose estimation have been proposed based on the latest AI achievements. However, such advancements require further strong and efficient methods w.r.t. training data and AI-architectures, which are capable in synergy to tackle current challenges, precision limits, and scalability beyond domain gaps. In this paper, we discuss these current limits and trends in the related state-of-the-art which are challenging those. Further we discuss our current work in progress on bridging the domain gap between simulations and real world applications by linking those in the training data generation.

</details>


### [344] [Co-VLA: Coordination-Aware Structured Action Modeling for Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20285)
*Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Mingbo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Co-VLA通过引入显式结构先验（结构化动作专家SAE和潜在感知控制器LAC）来解决双机械臂紧耦合操作中隐含协调不足的问题，在协调任务中成功率提升27%，OOD场景性能翻倍，任务完成时间缩短25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在双臂操作中依赖端到端学习的隐式协调，但面对紧耦合任务和执行约束时，隐式协调无法保证可靠、可解释且稳定的行为。

Method: 提出Co-VLA框架：(1) 用结构化动作专家(SAE)替换原始VLA的动作头，通过模块化的协调感知损失构建共享潜在变量（编码协调意图）和残差潜在变量（捕获各臂执行调整）；(2) 部署时使用潜在感知控制器(LAC)实时调节同步强度、执行不对称性、平滑性和安全约束。

Result: 紧协调任务成功率提升27%，OOD真实场景从13%提升至27%，任务完成时间降低25%。

Conclusion: 在VLA模型中引入显式协调结构先验能显著提升双臂操作性能，且LAC可在关节命令层面兼容标准控制管线，无需力或阻抗控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong capabilities in single and dual-arm robotic manipulation. Prior works show coordinated bimanual behaviors can emerge from end-to-end learning, leveraging large vision-language backbones with continuous action prediction. However, as bimanual tasks become tightly coupled and execution constraints become critical, implicit coordination alone is insufficient to ensure reliable, interpretable, and stable behavior.
  In this work, we propose Co-VLA, a coordination-aware bimanual manipulation framework introducing explicit structural priors into VLA models. We instantiate our method on a state-of-the-art vision-language backbone by replacing its monolithic action head with a Structured Action Expert (SAE) designed for bimanual coordination. Specifically, we introduce explicit structure at the action generation level with a modular coordination-aware loss that shapes shared and residual latents according to task-specific structures. The shared latent encodes task-level coordination intent, while residual latents capture execution adjustments for each arm.
  At deployment, a Latent-Aware Controller (LAC) interprets the learned representations to modulate synchronization strength, execution asymmetry, smoothness, and safety constraints in real time. LAC operates at the joint-command level and remains compatible with standard control pipelines without requiring force or impedance control. Experiments across simulation and real-world benchmarks show Co-VLA significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, achieving a 27% success rate gain in tight-coordination tasks, more than doubling performance in OOD real-world scenarios (from 13% to 27%), and reducing task completion time by up to 25%.

</details>


### [345] [Towards 3D karst underwater scene reconstruction from rotating sonar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20322)
*Georgios Evangelos Margaritis,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou,Zhi Yan,Andreas Nüchter,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合连续时间SLAM轨迹校正和两阶段深度学习表面重建的管道，用于从声纳剖面仪数据重建水下岩溶通道，生成沉浸式可导航3D网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岩溶含水层提供关键淡水资源，但其复杂的地下几何结构难以理解，且存在显著 hazards；声纳数据稀疏嘈杂，导航估计存在漂移，标准3D重建方法受限。

Method: 采用连续时间SLAM方法校正轨迹漂移，结合新颖的两阶段深度学习方法进行表面重建，最终生成用于水文地质分析的沉浸式可导航3D网格。

Result: 成功从声纳剖面仪数据中重建了水下岩溶通道，校正了轨迹漂移，生成了高保真3D网格。

Conclusion: 该管道有效解决了水下岩溶通道重建中数据稀疏、噪声大和导航漂移的挑战，为水文地质分析提供了实用的3D可视化工具。

Abstract: Karst aquifers provide critical freshwater resources but pose significant hazards due to their complex and poorly understood subsurface geometry. Mapping these environments is challenging because sonar data from underwater exploration is sparse and noisy, while navigation estimates suffer from drift limiting standard 3D reconstruction methods. We present a pipeline for reconstructing underwater karst conduits from a sonar profiler. We combine a continuous-time SLAM approach to correct trajectory drift with a novel two-stage deep learning method for surface reconstruction, producing an immersive and navigable 3D mesh for hydrogeological analysis.

</details>


### [346] [Autonomous Driving with Priority-Ordered STL Specifications Under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20336)
*Taha Bouzid,Shuhao Qi,Mircea Lazar,Sofie Haesaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的轨迹规划框架，通过信号时序逻辑（STL）的字典序优先级排序处理安全关键场景下的多目标冲突，并结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制实现，在仿真中验证了其处理多模态不确定性下冲突目标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在安全关键场景中，并非总能同时满足安全性、乘客舒适度和交通规则遵守等多重要求，需要对目标进行优先级排序；同时，必须显式考虑周围交通参与者预测轨迹的不确定性。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知的轨迹规划框架，采用字典序优先级排序的STL规范（在不确定性下仍然有效），并基于MPPI控制实现该公式化方法。

Result: 在仿真场景中验证了该方法能有效处理存在多模态不确定性下的冲突目标。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在安全关键场景下高效处理冲突目标，同时显式考虑预测不确定性，但需要在实际场景中进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must plan trajectories that satisfy a multitude of requirements on safety, passenger comfort, and compliance with traffic rules. However, in safety-critical scenarios, it is not always possible to satisfy all requirements simultaneously, necessitating their prioritization based on importance. At the same time, in these safety-critical scenarios, the uncertainty in trajectory predictions of the surrounding traffic, such as other vehicles and pedestrians, should be explicitly accounted for. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware trajectory planning framework that incorporates a predefined lexicographic ordering over Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that stays valid under uncertainty. We implement this formulation with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control and we demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on simulation scenarios, showing that our framework efficiently handles conflicting objectives under realistic multi-modal uncertainty.

</details>


### [347] [An Infrastructure-less, Control-Independent Solution to Relative Localisation of a Team of Mobile Robots using Ranging Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20365)
*Paolo Golinelli,Tommaso Faraci,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需锚点、完全去中心化的协作定位算法，仅依赖本地里程计、稀疏的机器人间测距和短距离通信，采用多假设贝叶斯框架维护所有可行解，在瞬态不可观测条件下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无固定基础设施、部署要求快速灵活且系统需求最小化的场景中，机器人团队需要一种去中心化的协作定位方法，现有方法大多需要控制机器人运动以保证团队可观测性，这限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出一种无需锚点的完全去中心化协作定位算法，采用多假设贝叶斯框架维护整个可行解集合，仅依赖本地里程计、稀疏的机器人间测距和短距离通信，通过信息共享使每个智能体受益于整个群体的估计，即使在部分连接条件下也能工作。

Result: 该方法在瞬态不可观测条件下保持鲁棒性，无需控制机器人运动即可保证团队可观测性，支持部分连接条件下的信息共享。

Conclusion: 该算法同时解决了无基础设施、快速灵活部署、最小系统需求等挑战，是一种实用的去中心化机器人团队定位方案。

Abstract: The ability to localise teams of robots is essential for applications ranging from robotic fleets in unstructured environments to cooperative control and navigation tasks. In such contexts, fixed infrastructure is often unavailable, deployments must be fast and flexible, and system requirements must be minimal. We present a decentralised cooperative localisation algorithm that addresses all these challenges at once. The method is anchor-less, fully decentralised, and, unlike most existing approaches, does not require controlling the robots motion to ensure team observability. It relies only on local odometry, sparse inter-agent ranging measurements, and short-range communication, all of which are widely available in practice. The algorithm adopts a multi-hypothesis Bayesian framework that maintains the entire set of feasible solutions, ensuring robustness under transient unobservable conditions. Moreover, through information sharing, each agent benefits from the estimates of the entire group, even in partially connected conditions.

</details>


### [348] [CoLI: A Reproducible Platform for Continuum Robot Learning via Monolithic 3D Printing and Isomorphic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20389)
*Ziyuan Tang,Chenxi Xiao**

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种开源连续体机器人平台，采用多材料3D打印实现整体式柔性结构制造，通过同构遥操作界面建立直接执行器级映射以消除运动学建模需求，并支持基于模仿学习的自主控制，实验验证了其可重复性和学习就绪特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因高自由度、柔性结构和操作安全性具有潜力，但其复杂的制造装配过程、困难运动学建模和缺乏直观控制界面导致可重复性问题，阻碍了研究与应用推广。

Method: 1) 多材料3D打印简化制造流程，实现整体式柔性结构（最小化装配）；2) 同构遥操作界面，直接执行器级映射，无需显式运动学建模，无奇异性；3) 支持基于模仿学习的自主控制。

Result: 通过硬件表征和操作任务实验，验证了该平台具有可重复性和学习就绪特性。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为连续体机器人社区提供了可重复、学习就绪的系统，能够加速算法开发和系统化基准测试。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer strong potential for manipulation tasks due to their high degrees of freedom, compliant structures, and operational safety. However, their adoption in both research and practical applications has been hindered by reproducibility issues arising from complex fabrication and assembly processes, challenging kinematic modeling, and a lack of intuitive control interfaces. To address these challenges, we present a novel open-source continuum robot design. The platform features a simplified fabrication pipeline enabled by multi-material 3D printing, allowing the arm to be fabricated as a monolithic compliant structure with minimal assembly. Control is achieved through an isomorphic teleoperation interface that establishes a direct actuator-level mapping, eliminating the need for explicit kinematic modeling and providing a singularity-free mapping. Building on this hardware design, the platform further supports imitation-learning-based autonomous control. The proposed system is evaluated through hardware characterization and a set of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the platform provides a reproducible, learning-ready continuum robot system, accelerating algorithmic development and systematic benchmarking for the continuum robotics community.

</details>


### [349] [Agentic AutoResearch forSpace Autonomy: An Auditable, LLM-Driven Research Agent for Aerospace Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20394)
*Amit Jain,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch框架，利用大语言模型作为离线研究代理，自主迭代优化航天器控制策略，并通过内置可信层（包括种子噪声测量、重新验证和剪枝检查）对结果进行认证。在交会和对接两个问题上，该方法显著优于无定向搜索，尤其是对接问题中无定向搜索无法找到可行策略，而AutoResearch学到的策略在所有随机种子下均满足安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器制导、导航与控制中基于学习策略的开发需要大量实验迭代，研究人员难以区分真实改进与种子噪声。现有方法缺乏自动化和可信度保障，需要一种能自主驱动研究循环并认证结果的框架。

Method: AutoResearch框架：大语言模型作为离线研究代理，读取问题描述和运行历史，每次迭代提出一个编辑操作，修改训练脚本并执行；内置可信层对每个结果进行三道检查（种子噪声校准、重新验证、留一剪枝）。

Result: 在Clohessy-Wiltshire交会问题和安全约束避碰对接任务上，AutoResearch学到的策略远超种子噪声阈值（多个标准差）；无定向搜索在相同参数空间内无法达到同等效果，对接任务中无定向搜索零可行策略，而AutoResearch在所有种子上均满足约束。

Conclusion: AutoResearch框架能有效自动化航天器控制策略的研究迭代过程，产生经过认证的可信结果，显著优于盲目搜索，尤其在强约束问题上展现出决定性优势。

Abstract: Spacecraft guidance, navigation, and control functions are increasingly realized as learned policies distilled from expert solvers. Developing such a policy is itself a research process: an investigator selects an architecture and hyperparameters, runs experiments, and must determine whether an apparent improvement is genuine or merely seed noise. This paper presents AutoResearch, a framework in which a large language model autonomously drives that loop for aerospace control problems, coupled with a credibility layer, built into the loop, that certifies each reported result against the problem's own measured seed noise. The language model serves only as the offline research agent that develops the control policy; the trained policy it produces is then deployed onboard the spacecraft, while the model itself never operates the vehicle. At each iteration the agent reads a plain-language problem description and the run history, proposes a single edit to the training script, executes it, and logs the outcome. No reported result is credited until it passes the same three checks: measured per-problem seed noise, reseeded verification of the best configuration, and leave-one-out pruning of the agent's edits. The same loop is applied, unchanged, to two aerospace control problems: a Clohessy-Wiltshire relative rendezvous and a safety-constrained collision-avoidance docking past a keep-out zone, each calibrated against a known optimal control benchmark. In both, the audited policy clears the measured seed noise by many standard deviations; an undirected search over the same parameters does not. On the docking problem the gap becomes categorical: undirected search yields no feasible policy, while the learned policy stays outside the keep-out zone on every seed.

</details>


### [350] [LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20424)
*Shikuan Shi,Chunran Zheng,Jiaming Xu,Tianyong Ye,Tao Yu,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIT-GS提出了一种基于LiDAR-惯性-热成像的高斯泼溅框架，通过引入LiDAR平面几何约束，在弱纹理和光照变化场景下显著提升了神经渲染的几何精度和渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-惯性-视觉(LIV)高斯地图构建方法在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下表现脆弱，原因是它们依赖RGB光度线索，缺乏可靠的几何约束。

Method: 1) 将LIV视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点，建立可靠的热成像-LiDAR关联；2) 在光束法平差中引入加权LiDAR点到平面残差，在弱热成像监督下联合优化相机姿态和3D点；3) 提出LiDAR平面正则化的可微分泼溅目标函数，约束渲染3D点与局部观测平面对齐，减少低对比度热成像中的表面增厚和结构漂移。

Result: 在自采序列和公开数据集上，LIT-GS在几何精度和渲染质量上持续优于现有基于LIV的高斯泼溅基线方法，尤其在挑战性的光照条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过注入LiDAR平面几何作为显式约束，可以有效解决热成像弱纹理场景下的结构漂移问题，实现鲁棒的多模态高斯地图构建。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [351] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy是一个基于有限元法的视觉触觉传感器仿真框架，集成于Isaac Sim平台，通过UIPC求解器从超弹性本构定律直接计算Cauchy应力张量，提供基于第一性原理的力学真实值。该框架支持自动网格生成与自适应细化，模块化接口可快速集成多种传感器（GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact），在60个并行环境中达到555 FPS聚合吞吐量，应力提取开销低于1 ms。物理验证实验中，仿真与真实触觉响应在1.2556 N至4.7332 N力范围内SSIM超过0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的触觉传感器仿真方法难以在GPU加速的机器人平台上提供准确的机械应力场，缺乏基于物理原理的力计算能力，阻碍了强化学习所需的高保真仿真。

Method: 整合UIPC求解器，从超弹性本构定律直接计算Cauchy应力张量，投影至接触面获得牵引力和压力分布；采用几何感知自适应细化的自动网格生成；设计模块化传感器接口。

Result: 单环境33.40 FPS，60并行环境555 FPS聚合吞吐量，应力提取开销<1 ms；物理验证中SSIM>0.93（力范围1.2556-4.7332 N）。

Conclusion: TaCauchy能够为下游机器人操作任务提供准确、基于物理的力监督，弥合了仿真与真实触觉响应之间的差距。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [352] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的块坐标下降框架，结合自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新和最小加权协方差行列式估计器，实现了在含异常值情况下同时估计状态和协方差的自调优方案，无需手动参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器测量常受异常值和非高斯噪声污染，导致经典状态估计器产生有偏且不可靠的估计。现有鲁棒估计器能降权异常值但不进行测量协方差估计，而联合状态与协方差估计器则假设高斯残差和固定损失形状参数。将两者整合到统一框架中是一个重要的研究机会。

Method: 提出统一的块坐标下降(BCD)框架，包含三个核心组件：范数感知的自适应鲁棒损失函数、迭代重加权最小二乘(IRLS)状态更新、以及最小加权协方差行列式(MCD)协方差估计器，三者交替优化实现自调优联合估计。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛仿真和实际超宽带(UWB)定位实验中，所提方法一致地恢复了真实内点测量协方差，在状态估计精度上达到或超过了所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架能够在不需手动参数调整的情况下，同时在异常值存在时可靠地估计状态和协方差，兼具鲁棒性和自调优能力。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [353] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-Planner接口，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从锚点规划器的候选轨迹中选择最优索引，并通过基于几何相似性与指数衰减的轨迹级融合层（Score Fusion）解决VLM查询延迟（1-3秒）与实时控制（5-20Hz）之间的冲突。在约2000个具有挑战性的真实场景中，VLM选择将ADE降低30%，且在模拟中面对高达5秒延迟仍保持>80%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的侧边导航规划器在挑战性场景（如路口、行人遭遇）中存在轨迹评分差距，即规划器选出的最佳轨迹并非候选集中最优，这源于规划器高层场景理解能力不足。

Method: 1）VLM-Planner接口：VLM从规划器候选轨迹集中选择候选索引；2）训练-free的轨迹级融合层（Score Fusion）：通过候选轨迹与VLM选择之间的几何相似性结合指数衰减，将过时的VLM选择实时转化为规划器评分信号，弥补延迟间隙。

Result: 在约2000个挑战性真实场景中，VLM选择相比规划器自身最优选择实现30%的ADE降低；在模拟中，Score Fusion在高达5秒延迟下仍保持>80%的成功率；完整系统在校园复杂侧边环境中成功导航。

Conclusion: VLM-Planner接口在不更换原有规划器的情况下，显著提升了挑战性场景下的轨迹选择质量，所提的延迟弹性融合机制有效解决了VLM推理速度与实时控制需求之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [354] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GroundControl，一种轨迹一致的不确定性估计方法，通过卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标动力学偏差来预测VLN智能体的导航失败，并在选择性风险-覆盖协议（SRCN）下显著优于熵、共形和启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航智能体在基准任务上表现良好，但常因振荡、停滞或低效绕行等可预测的轨迹级故障而失败。可靠部署需要能在执行过程中预判故障的不确定性信号，而非仅反映瞬时动作熵。

Method: 提出GroundControl：使用常速度卡尔曼滤波器建模距离演化，结合归一化创新统计量与进度、单调性、路径效率和振荡行为等轨迹特征，构建轨迹一致的不确定性分数。同时形式化选择性风险-覆盖导航（SRCN）协议，通过风险-覆盖曲线和AURC/E-AURC指标评估不确定性质量。

Result: 在5个EB-Navigation分割（300个episode）上，轨迹一致不确定性在基于成功的选择性风险下达到接近oracle的排序效果，GPT-4o模型的加权平均E-AURC_SR=0.0024，显著优于基线。在SPL选择性评估下，GroundControl在所有模型和分割上均取得最低AURC和E-AURC。

Conclusion: 建模与目标导向动力学的偏差能为VLN智能体提供可解释且鲁棒的故障预判信号，轨迹一致的不确定性估计是部署可信导航智能体的关键工具。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [355] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeLNN是一种基于液态神经网络和MobileNetV3的超轻量级视觉扫描路径预测模型，仅需0.61 GFLOPs即可实现SOTA性能，计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍，并成功集成到空中机器人的主动相机控制策略中，实现了类人注意力引导的自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉通过结构化扫描路径高效处理场景，但将这一机制赋予机器人自主系统仍处于初级阶段，且现有预测模型计算成本过高，难以满足实时部署需求。

Method: 提出GazeLNN架构，以液态神经网络（LNN）作为循环引擎，MobileNetV3用于特征提取；采用自回归方式，根据当前视觉刺激和注视历史顺序预测注视热力图。

Result: 在MIT Low Resolution数据集上达到0.47 ScanMatch分数，超越现有循环基线模型；计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍；集成至强化学习训练的主动相机-机器人控制策略中，并在真实空中机器人上成功部署验证。

Conclusion: GazeLNN证明了高效计算与类人视觉注意力建模的可行结合，为资源受限的机器人平台提供了实用且性能优越的扫描路径预测方案，推动了人类注意力建模在机器人自主性中的实际应用。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [356] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了弹性连续体机器人的运动规划，通过在遗传算法和A*算法中引入层次分析法（考虑距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度四个标准）进行多准则路径决策。结果表明，遗传算法相比A*算法性能不受环境基数影响，能生成更多样化路径，提升机器人弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过多准则决策优化路径规划算法，延长连续体机器人的维护周期，提升其弹性（resilience）。

Method: 将层次分析法（AHP）集成到遗传算法和A*算法中，基于距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度四个准则评估路径质量，在两种模拟环境中进行实验验证。

Result: 遗传算法的执行时间不随环境基数变化，能生成比A*算法更多样化的路径，从而提升机器人弹性。

Conclusion: 多准则路径规划，特别是基于遗传算法的方法，能有效提升连续体机器人的弹性性能。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [357] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动框架，利用超过400万帧人类日常操作中的指尖运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置生成机器人手设计。框架生成了6自由度通用手和低自由度任务专用手，并采用强化学习加速设计搜索（从数小时缩短至数分钟）。实物实验表明，6自由度手在遥操作指尖跟踪精度上优于现有商用机器人手，专用手则以更低机械复杂度复现了结构化人类轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制方面进展迅速，但物理身体（硬件设计）的学习仍然困难，因为设计与控制的联合搜索是一个巨大的组合优化问题。现有方法难以高效地从人类演示数据中直接生成机器人手的具体物理形态。

Method: 1) 利用超过400万帧人类日常操作中的指尖运动数据作为参考；2) 采用逆运动学匹配指尖位置作为统一的简单控制策略；3) 优化树状结构的机器人手以复现目标运动；4) 使用强化学习智能体加速设计空间搜索，提出好的手部设计和关节角度；5) 直接制造为一体化铰接结构（打印即用关节）。

Result: 6自由度通用手在遥操作指尖跟踪精度上优于现有商用机器人手；3自由度任务专用手以更低机械复杂度成功复现了结构化人类及合成轨迹；强化学习将设计搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。

Conclusion: 大规模人类运动数据不仅可以用于训练机器人控制器，还可以作为优化和生成机器人物理形态的参考依据，证明了数据驱动方法在机器人硬件设计中的可行性。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [358] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM提出了一种结合近期帧、事件边界锚点帧和紧凑摘要token的混合记忆设计，通过定制化注意力机制实现高效长程记忆检索，在长时域、记忆依赖的机器人操作任务中优于强VLA和WAM基线方法，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在非马尔可夫环境中面临根本性权衡：高效推理方法仅依赖有限窗口的近期观测，难以处理记忆依赖任务；而保留完整历史的方法随序列长度增加导致时间和空间成本显著增长。

Method: MemoryWAM采用混合记忆设计，包含三种记忆组件：近期帧（短时细节）、事件边界锚点帧（关键事件标记）和紧凑摘要token（长程历史压缩表示），并设计了定制化注意力机制以同时检索详细短时上下文和压缩长时上下文。

Result: 在长时域、记忆依赖的机器人操作任务（仿真和真实世界）中，MemoryWAM在性能上优于强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基线和现有WAM基线，同时推理延迟和GPU内存使用更低。

Conclusion: 通过高效的持久记忆设计，MemoryWAM成功解决了WAM在非马尔可夫环境中的历史-效率权衡问题，为长时域机器人操作提供了一种兼顾性能与计算效率的可行范式。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [359] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HUG，一种基于流匹配的模型，能从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化的人手抓取姿态，并重定向到机器人手部实现零样本抓取。同时发布了包含100万帧、6707个物体实例的抓取数据集HUGs和包含90个物体的基准HUG-Bench，在真实世界测试中比基线方法提升23%-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人抓取远不及人类水平，现有方法缺乏多样化、自然的抓取数据。人类每天抓取数千物体，是最自然的抓取数据来源，因此需要从人类抓取数据中学习通用抓取模型。

Method: 使用智能眼镜收集100万帧第一人称抓取数据集1M-HUGs；提出基于流匹配的模型，融合RGB和深度观测，输出手腕平移、手腕旋转和MANO手部姿态参数化的抓取；将预测抓取重定向到不同机器人手部。

Result: 构建含90个未见物体的HUG-Bench基准，涵盖5种几何类别和多种尺寸；在30个物体的真实世界测试中，HUG比最先进抓取基线方法提升23%和34%。

Conclusion: HUG通过从人类抓取数据学习，实现了对任意物体的多样化抓取生成，并可零样本迁移到多种机器人平台上，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [360] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRei，一个基于道义策略的治理框架，用于约束由大语言模型驱动的自主智能体系统。该框架利用OWL本体语言和外部高性能逻辑引擎，支持许可/禁止约束、义务生命周期管理、豁免（dispensation）、策略冲突解决以及领域本体推理等企业治理需求，弥补了XACML、Rego、Cedar等现有策略引擎的不足，并可与A2AS等行业标准框架自然集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体系统能够调用工具、操作数据、安装软件并与跨组织边界的同级智能体协作，因此需要比传统身份验证和访问控制更完整的企业治理约束。现有策略引擎（如XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅支持许可/禁止子集，缺乏义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、特定场景下的义务豁免以及领域本体推理能力，无法满足安全、隐私和合规方面的治理需求。

Method: 基于Rei框架的道义策略语言，以OWL（Web Ontology Language）表达策略规则，并在LLM外部由高性能逻辑引擎在运行时进行评估。该管道同时管控智能体的工具调用和智能体间的消息通信。支持义务（obligations）、豁免（dispensations）、策略冲突解决以及基于本体类层次结构的推理。

Result: 通过示例展示了道义策略能够捕获现有生产级策略引擎（XACML、Rego、Cedar）无法表达的安全和隐私治理约束。AgenticRei与A2AS等行业标准框架自然兼容。

Conclusion: AgenticRei通过道义策略实现了对LLM驱动自主智能体系统的全面治理，填补了现有策略引擎在义务管理、豁免、冲突解决和本体推理方面的空白，为自主智能体的安全、隐私与合规治理提供了一套可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [361] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协作的流水线方法，用于衡量计算机科学本科课程对国际课程指南（CS2013和CS2023）的覆盖程度。通过对一个认证项目的纵向分析，发现项目对两个版本指南的知识单元覆盖率稳定在约50%，但CS2023提高了能力期望，导致认知深度满足率从95%降至76%。研究还区分了持续存在的结构性差距与指南演进带来的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前本科计算机科学课程虽有约十年一轮的国际指南，但缺乏可靠、可重复的方法来量化课程对指南的覆盖程度，以及指南更新时覆盖情况的变化。

Method: 提出人机协同流水线：将课程和指南分别构建为结构化语料库，通过语义检索生成课程与知识单元的候选匹配，再通过人工判断确认。使用七种检索器进行基准测试，采用倒数秩融合集成（最佳），对比了长上下文模型与小句子模型的表现。通过独立第二评分者进行验证（Cohen's kappa分别为0.64和0.69）。

Result: 项目覆盖CS2023的49.7%和CS2013的50.9%，覆盖率基本恒定。在覆盖的知识单元中，约88%有对应的能力表述，但CS2023下仅76%达到推荐认知深度（CS2013为95%）。发现了持续的结构性缺口（并行与分布式计算、编程语言基础、系统基础）。

Conclusion: 纵向比较能区分持续存在的结构性问题与指南演进带来的差异；工具可复用且可向作者索取。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [362] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对8种现代扩散语言模型在8个基准（涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识和结构化问题求解）上进行了系统实验分析，评估生成质量和计算效率，并研究了推理阶段关键因素（去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略）的影响，揭示了不同任务、架构和推理预算下的性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
